> **Sprint 6 (track WF, window + layout fix pack; DM [#285](../decisions-log.md) decision 1 → A, scope #286; session-sim-9 FAIL a, b and glitches 1–5).** Decided, not open: a window **reopened** by the user whose stored spot is now **covered** is re-placed, otherwise it keeps the user's spot (BL-38, #285 → A); the PC rule uses token **footprints**, and a first placement never lands mid-map over tokens while a clear spot over none exists (BL-39); the DM's pending-actions toast never covers the Fog bar or the map's top-left (BL-40, [FR-NOTE-42/43](notifications.md#6-functional-requirements)); Fit doesn't squeeze the map (BL-41, [FR-MAP-80/81](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)); the players' east third is not under the sheet at 1280×800 (BL-42). Root causes (code + sim-9 notes): user opens skip the overlap check (`manager.ts` `open`: only `by: "feature"` re-places); `clearOfPcs` shrinks a window to a PC's **centre** (sim-9: Compendium's left edge = Mira's centre x 164); edge-before-inward ranks above token cover, so a shrunk edge slot over 6 tokens beat a clear token-free slot at 576,60; players' fits frame only the tokens seen at that moment and nothing refits when more come into sight (probe, §12). New FR-SHELL-56–62; amended FR-SHELL-08, -41, -45, -46, -47. **For the DM:** OQ-SHELL-41 (the "covered" test), -42 (footprint margin), -43 (players' sheet at 1280×800); Fit's trade-off is [OQ-MAP-57](scenes-map.md#15-open-questions) (OQ-SHELL-40 moved there); the toast vs the Fog bar is [OQ-NOTE-25](notifications.md#15-open-questions). Everything that follows one of those picks is tagged **(proposed, OQ-…)**: the means are open, only the outcomes above are decided. One fix-pack walkthrough **F0–F13** in §13 here.

**Sprint 7 root causes** (code read; each "confirmed by" is the builder's first test)

| Symptom (sim-10) | Suspected cause | Confirmed by |
|---|---|---|
| Windows over PCs (b02, b03, r06) | `autoPlace` (`manager.ts`) auto-minimises only while the best rect is not clear of windows (`p.clear`); a clear rect over a PC wins (rank field 5 sits after the window fields) | `FR-SHELL-77 auto-minimise instead of covering a PC` |
| Reset layout shrinks the player's Initiative to 280×226 (c05) | `reset` runs `defaultLayout` with the drawn tokens (`placementTokens`): the sheet leaves its zone, Initiative takes a shrunk leftover | `FR-SHELL-80 reset keeps the zones` |
| Pushed windows stay pushed (c05) | `pushWindows` writes the pushed rect and keeps no way back (FR-SHELL-55 "not restored", by design then) | `FR-SHELL-81 pushed windows return` |
| Toast over a pushed Scenes (b05) | the DM box is soft, so a push may end in the band; the stack's last choice is "the least window body" | `FR-NOTE-44 toast clear of a pushed window` |
| Space doesn't press buttons (BL-37) | `MapCanvas.tsx`'s window-level Space branch swallows Space for non-`<button>` controls always and for buttons while the pointer is over the map, even with keyboard focus | `FR-SHELL-83 Space presses buttons` (both directions) |

> **Sprint 8 (track WF; DM [#333](../decisions-log.md), [#334](../decisions-log.md)).** Decided (#333): **one placement path**. At small screens PCs stay clear first, then windows shrink, then the least recently used launcher window is put away. "Not below their useful size unless nothing else works" is [sprint-8](../agents/sprint-8.md)'s reading of #333; the DM confirmed it at gate 1 (#335). PCs are player characters only (#318). It is proven by a session-replay harness. Fixes BL-52, -53, -59, -60, -61, -64, -65, -66, -70. New FR-SHELL-95–119; FR-SHELL-118 lists what it retires. Proposed ADR: [one placer](../architecture/adr/0060-one-window-placer.md). Decided at DM gate 1 (#335): OQ-SHELL-58–64 and OQ-MAP-63 as picked; the ADR accepted. Seams S1–S5, S12, S14 and review-b rulings 1–15, 26 applied. 0 B.

**Five paths today → one placer** (code read at spec time)

| Path | Today | Breaks | Now |
|---|---|---|---|
| Open / appear | `autoPlace` → `placeWindow` | accepts a mid-map inward spot before putting a window away (BL-70); the 12-px gap loses a 321-px PC-clear strip (BL-64) | ladder, zones, 4-px gap fallback |
| Reopen | `clearAround`: bare `placeWindow`, no floor | neighbours written at min | ladder |
| Push / return | `pushWindows`: no PCs; pushes only on a new bar rect | BL-61, BL-52 | ladder; push on drag-end / re-select |
| Resize | `followDefaults`: no PCs, no floor, **written** | Game log stuck at its 300×160 min (BL-60) | displace down, return / grow back up |
| Refits | `pcsPan` re-scales **up** to 48 px; the fit's PC = any owned token | reveal zooms in (BL-65) | after the placer, never zooms in ([FR-MAP-93–98](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)) |

BL-59 → [FR-NOTE-51](notifications.md#6-functional-requirements); BL-53 → FR-SHELL-109; BL-66 → useful sizes (FR-SHELL-100, [FR-STATS-66](stats.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-CHARX-83](character-sheet-full.md#6-functional-requirements)).

State lives on the campaign row ([FR-CAMP-46–50](campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements)), files in [assets-pipeline](assets-pipeline.md) FR-ASSET-52–61; this spec owns the card, suppression, the DM's Pause control and playback by alert key: FR-SHELL-125–136. > **Sprint 9 (track PM, Party Manager + window memory; owner spec-pm; DM [#338](../decisions-log.md)).** Decided, not open: **window memory** (amends [ADR-0060](../architecture/adr/0060-one-window-placer.md) / #333): the placer places only a window the user hasn't moved or resized yet, and every window again after Reset layout. Once the user moves or resizes a window, it always reopens at exactly that size and spot, even over other windows or PCs, and is only nudged back on-screen if it would fall off the edge. The most recently opened / touched window goes on top. Nothing is shrunk or put away to make room for it. Tool-bar pushes stay temporary. **BL-85 superseded.** **Party Manager** ([party-board FR-PARTY-59–67](party-board.md#611-sprint-9-party-manager-track-pm)): `party.board` is renamed, gets Party status as its first tab, and goes to 550 px wide; the `party.summary` window and its launcher button go (FR-SHELL-140–143 retired). Frame follow-ups owned here: **BL-82** (placer bench), **BL-86** (DM map zoomed after a resize round trip, [FR-MAP-113](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)), **BL-87** (Game log shrinks for good), **BL-80** (harness cells, launcher labels). New FR-SHELL-144–164; proposed ADR [window memory](../architecture/adr/proposed-s9-pm-window-memory.md). **For the DM:** OQ-SHELL-74–79. Seams: RS, SP, FX (OQ-SHELL-80–82). 0 B on the wire (§12).

| FR-SHELL-159 | BL-87: a squeezed protected window grows back | PM1 | `FR-SHELL-159 Game log grows back (BL-87)` |
| FR-SHELL-160, -161 | BL-80: harness sets without `party.summary`; launcher labels (reasoned drop) | PM1, PM2 | `FR-SHELL-115 replay …`, `FR-SHELL-161 launcher threshold` |
| FR-SHELL-162–164 | Harness `memory` path + I9–I13; goldens; test hooks | PM1 | `FR-SHELL-115 replay <set> <camera> @<size> memory` |

## 1. Summary

- The one play page per client (Roll20-style): scene canvas as the base, **floating windows** over it ([ADR-0014](../architecture/adr/0014-mvp-scope.md), [decisions-log](../decisions-log.md) #44, #48). Replaces the DM's six-window setup ([vision](../vision.md#the-table-today)).
- Features don't own pages: they register **window types**; the shell opens, arranges and persists them. Plug-in contract: [ADR-0025](../architecture/adr/0025-play-shell-windows.md).
- **Layout** lives in browser storage, per campaign and owner (DM or the link's player); it never reaches the server (#121).
- Hosts the foundation UI: connection indicator, reconnect banner, presence ([live-sync](live-sync.md)); switcher, players panel, login / waiting / invalid-link screens ([campaign-management](campaign-management.md)).
- **Feedback pass** (F1, #148; [ADR-0030](../architecture/adr/0030-ui-notification-sounds.md)): an **alert** surface for features (screen flash + short UI sound + Toast); first caller: combat's player turn alert ([FR-CBT-41](combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements)). Per-browser mute + volume.
- **Playtest pass** (#171; FR-SHELL-29–33): one shared **tooltip** delay (≈ 400 ms) and no tooltip fighting a popover (P1); windows **fit the work area** on smaller viewports (G3); the DM's Initiative default shows a full encounter (G2); presence reads "unknown" while the DM is not live (G10).
- **Polish pass** (sprint 1; FR-SHELL-34, 35): the shell reports the **free area** (work area no window covers) so the map can frame tokens there (R1, #178); the DM's Game log shows a real history at default size (R3).
- **Sprint 2 phase 1** (P2b, N1; FR-SHELL-36–38, decided #190, [OQ-SHELL-21](#15-open-questions)): minimise gets a meaning of its own. A minimised window is **parked**: it stays alive (scroll, tab, drafts kept), its launcher button looks different from an open one, and a dot shows when something new happened in it. Close stays "gone".
- **Sprint 4** (FR-SHELL-40–44): windows that open **by themselves** go where they cover the fewest tokens, and a feature never opens one on top of another (FR-SHELL-40, 41); target-less windows (the handout viewer) don't come back after a reload (FR-SHELL-42); the launcher fits any viewport ≥ 1280 px (FR-SHELL-43); the shell offers every free rectangle so the map picks the one its tokens fit (FR-SHELL-44 → [FR-MAP-58](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)). Browser-only, 0 bytes.
- **Sprint 6** (FR-SHELL-56–62): a window you **reopen** goes back to your spot unless something now covers it; then it is placed like a new one (decided, #285 A; what "covered" means is proposed, OQ-SHELL-41). Auto placement keeps PC **token footprints** clear (decided; the margin is proposed, OQ-SHELL-42) and prefers a spot over no token at all. Proposed, pending the DM: the DM's toasts stay home (top-centre), off the tool bar (OQ-NOTE-25 A); the Fit keeps only the summary strip and whatever is showing clear, not the whole empty toast box (OQ-MAP-57 D); a player's map refits when new tokens come into sight under a window (OQ-SHELL-43 A). Browser-only, 0 bytes.
- **Sprint 5** (FR-SHELL-45–54): the screen has **zones**: right column = Initiative (DM) / sheet (player), bottom strip = a **wide Game log**, toasts and roll popups where they are today (players: bottom-right **toast corner**; DM: **top-centre**, #256), top strip right of the tool rail = the active **tool bar**, which pushes windows off it (FR-SHELL-55), the rest = map. Windows the user opens go to a clear spot (edges first, then beside an edge window, #256 amending #162), never on a window or a keep-out region while one exists; when the screen is full the least recently used launcher window is auto-minimised. The fit keeps every token visible ([FR-MAP-59](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)). Browser-only, 0 bytes.
- **Sprint 6** (track PF, FR-SHELL-70–76): a **Preferences window** opened from a top-right **identity chip** (colour dot + your name). Features register **preference sections** in it (colour, sound, dice, layout, …); each setting keeps its storage (browser or server). The player's top-bar speaker retires; its controls live in the Sound section.
- **Sprint 7** (track FU, FR-SHELL-77–88): a window never lands on a PC while another launcher window could be **auto-minimised** instead (#310 A); a window is auto-minimised before it would shrink below a readable size (proposed); Reset layout puts the default windows back in their zones and refits the map; a window a tool bar pushed goes back when the tool closes (proposed); pushes stay out of the DM's toast band; Space and keyboard focus work as on any web page. Browser-only, 0 bytes.
- **Sprint 8** (track WF, FR-SHELL-95–119): one placer for opens, reopens, pushes, resizes and the map's refits, with one rule (#333): PCs clear, then shrink to the useful size, then put away the least recently used launcher window. Default windows keep their zones (#335). What the shell moved for a tool or a small screen comes back. Proven by a session-replay harness. 0 B.

## 2. Scope

- Page frame for DM and player clients: base layer, top bar, tool rail slot, launcher, banner, toast region. Window manager: open, close, move, resize, bring to front, minimise; clamp to viewport; reset layout *(proposed)*.
- Window registry (client code) and `openWindow(kind, instance?)` for features; `instance` key ready for several windows of one type (tokens open notes later, ADR-0014), MVP uses `main` only.
- **Alerts** (FR-SHELL-22–27): `alert()` API, flash, built-in ping, player sound control (mute, volume), audio unlock on first interaction, test-mode hooks.
- **Playtest pass** (FR-SHELL-29–33): shared Tooltip rules for both clients; render-time fit of windows to the work area; DM Initiative default height; presence badge while not live.
- **Polish pass** (FR-SHELL-34, 35): free-area helper for the canvas; Game log history numbers at default size. No new window, default geometry or stored field.
- **Sprint 2 phase 1** (FR-SHELL-36–38, decided #190, OQ-SHELL-21): minimised windows stay mounted; distinct launcher states; optional per-window-type `activity` for a "new" dot; *(S2)* opt-in `growToContent` (FR-SHELL-39). No new window or default geometry; `WindowState` gains only optional `fixedH`.
- **Sprint 4** (FR-SHELL-40–44): token-aware auto placement; feature-driven opens re-placed off other windows; `restoreOpen` on window types; launcher compaction + overflow menu; candidate free rectangles. No new stored field; `WindowDef` gains optional `restoreOpen`; `openWindow` gains an optional `by`.
- **Sprint 5** (FR-SHELL-45–54): placement rank fix, inward columns, screen-full fallback, keep-out regions (toast regions + tool bar), toast boxes sized for the 320 × 190 popup, tool bars pushing windows aside, named default layouts with viewport-dependent sizes, growth cap, old-default migration, re-baselined coverage floors. `WindowDef` gains optional `defaultSize` function and `previousDefaults`; nothing stored or sent changes. Out: window contents (Creatures names cut to "G…" in a narrow window, sim-8 j: entities), the combat apply popup's spot (combat-tracker, track R).
- **Sprint 6** (FR-SHELL-56–62): reopen rule, footprint PC rule, token-free rank, the DM's shown-stack rect for the Fit, the player 1280×800 outcome, the placement contract for CP's / PF's new windows, deterministic layout tests. `ui-window` `data-placed` gains `reopened`; nothing stored or sent changes. Out: window contents (each owner), the Preferences window itself and the top-right area (PF, after WF1), the companion card (CP); pushed windows staying pushed after the tool closes (accepted, sprint-5 playtest).
- **Sprint 6** (track PF, FR-SHELL-70–76): identity chip in the top bar; window type `shell.prefs`; preference-section registries (one per client); Sound and Layout sections. Out: the colour picker itself ([campaign-management](campaign-management.md) FR-CAMP-35), dice settings' content ([dice-extras](dice-extras.md) FR-DICEX-57), new settings (none added), syncing device settings across browsers (OQ-SHELL-50), placement / keep-outs (track WF).
- **Sprint 7** (track FU, FR-SHELL-77–88): auto-minimising rule, auto-minimise order, useful size, Reset layout zones, pushed windows returning, pushes clear of the DM toast band, Space and keyboard-focus Tooltips, the size table, the placement contract for ST's Stats window, auto-minimising invariants + sim-10 replays in the layout tests. `WindowDef` gains optional `usefulSize`; client memory gains a pushed window's pre-push rect; the stored `WindowState` gains optional `userSpot` only if OQ-SHELL-55 B (old entries parse); nothing sent changes. Out: window contents (each owner: the companion card's size CHARX, the encounter editor CBT, Creatures ENT, the concentration chip CBTX), the toast stack itself (NOTE), the map camera (MAP).
- **Sprint 8** (track WF, FR-SHELL-95–119): placer, zones, ladder, useful sizes, displacement memory, push triggers, rail inset, registries for TT / SB / CB, the harness. `WindowState` gains optional `userSize`. Out: window contents, the toast code (NOTE), camera maths (MAP), the TT / SB / CB surfaces themselves.
- **Sprint 9** (track PM, FR-SHELL-144–164): window memory (remembered windows reopen exactly, MRU on top, nothing makes room, pushes temporary), Reset forgets, `absorbs` migration, Party Manager's row, `party.summary` removed, BL-80 / -82 / -87 (BL-86 in scenes-map). No new stored field. Out: SP's palette and shortcuts, RS's windows, FX's toast runtime.
- **Later, not built** (nothing here may block them): command palette, shortcuts, portrait strip, pause overlay, DM "view as player". They plug in via the registry, a reserved strip slot above the launcher and an overlay layer.

## 3. Goals

- Everything used during play is reachable without leaving the canvas; a player sees the active scene with no clicks after opening the link.
- Each owner's arrangement survives reload, reconnect and a server or browser crash. A feature adds a window by registering it, no shell code changes. Layouts never leave the browser; opening a window grants no data.

## 4. Non-goals

- BG2 styling, portrait strip (→ [theme-system](post-mvp/theme-system.md)); mobile/touch ([ADR-0007](../architecture/adr/0007-product-scope.md)); docked panels; pop-out browser windows; restoring a layout on another browser (#121).
- Window contents (each owning spec); canvas behaviour ([scenes-map](scenes-map.md)).
- Music, ambience, soundboard, voice ([ADR-0007](../architecture/adr/0007-product-scope.md), [ADR-0030](../architecture/adr/0030-ui-notification-sounds.md)); DM-added sounds (tier A, design for later, FR-SHELL-26); OS / push notifications; a DM sound control (no DM alerts in MVP).

## 5. User stories

- **DM:** I want the players panel, scene list and (later) initiative as windows over the map, so that I run the session from one tab; and my arrangement back after a crash, so that I don't rebuild it mid-session.
- **Player:** I want to land on the map the DM is showing, so that I'm in the game the moment I open my link; and my windows where I left them.
- **Player:** I want the screen to flash and ping when something needs me (my turn), and to mute or turn it down, so that I don't miss it and it doesn't annoy the table.
- *(Sprint 4)* **DM:** When combat starts I want Initiative to open where it doesn't hide the goblins I'm about to target, and never on top of the window I'm reading. **Both:** on a 1280-wide laptop I want every launcher button reachable and the tokens I can see out from under my windows.
- *(Sprint 6)* **DM:** When I close Compendium and open it again after resizing or opening other windows, I want it back where I put it, unless that spot is now taken, and never on top of another window or my PCs. When a combat card waits, I still want every Fog bar control. **Player:** On my laptop, when the door opens and goblins come into sight, I want them on screen, not under my sheet.
- *(Sprint 7)* **DM:** When I reopen Encounters at 1440×900 I want it never on top of Mira, Thorn and Brakka; if there's no room, put away a window I'm not using instead. **Player:** After Reset layout I want my sheet and Initiative where they start, full size; when the AoE panel closes I want my sheet back where it was. **Both:** Tab to a button and press Space, like any web page.
- *(Sprint 8)* **DM:** at 1280×720, windows I open are readable and never on Mira; a resize and back restores my layout. **Player:** Reveal all with the card open zooms out, not in.
- *(Sprint 9)* **DM:** once I've dragged Compendium where I like it, it opens there every time, on top, even over the map's PCs, and my other windows don't jump to make room; Reset layout starts over. **Player:** my sheet stays where I moved it after a reload or a smaller screen.
- *(Sprint 5)* **DM:** When I open Compendium, Party and Encounters during a fight, I want each one where I can read it, not stacked on the last one or on my Game log; and a Game log wide enough that a damage line fits on one line. My roll popups and combat cards should not sit on the monsters at the top of the map. **Player:** On my laptop I want my sheet, Initiative and the AoE panel side by side with the map, not on top of each other or of the monsters.

## 6. Functional requirements

| ID | Requirement | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| FR-SHELL-01 | Each client has one play page. Base layer fills the viewport. Stacking, bottom → top: base layer < windows < chrome (top bar, tool rail at the left edge with content from scenes-map, launcher) < reconnect banner, toasts < dialogs, overlays. **Toast region:** bottom-right for players; top-centre for the DM, below the top bar and below top-anchored window title bars (so a sticky card never covers a window's close / minimise buttons), clear of the right-edge Initiative rows (#153, #165). *(Sprint 5, decided #256, OQ-SHELL-32 C)* Unchanged: players bottom-right, DM top-centre (#153). *(Sprint 5, settled by seams S4)* Layer order with the sprint-5 overlays: base < windows < chrome < reconnect banner, toasts and roll popups < dialogs, overlays — incl. party-board's **present popup** (a handout forced open, track P), above windows and toasts — < the dice layer (topmost, input-free, FR-DICEX-33). | #153, #165; s5 |
| FR-SHELL-02 | Player: valid link + active campaign → loading skeleton, then the shell after the first snapshot with the active scene as base layer. DM: base layer = the DM's viewed scene (#122, [FR-MAP-08](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)). Nothing to show → one empty-state component: player "Waiting for the DM to show a scene", DM "No scenes yet" (the DM sees the first scene when none is active, [FR-MAP-08](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements); CON-32). Windows still work. | [FR-CAMP-16](campaign-management.md#6-functional-requirements) |
| FR-SHELL-03 | DM: valid secret + active campaign → shell after the first snapshot. No active campaign → campaign-management's "Create your first campaign" page; no shell, no WS. | |
| FR-SHELL-04 | Login (DM), invalid-link (`rejected`) and waiting (`waiting`) screens replace the whole shell, with campaign-management's wording. Leaving `waiting` restores the shell without reload, windows as they were. | [FR-SYNC-24](live-sync.md#6-functional-requirements) |
| FR-SHELL-05 | Top bar, right: connection indicator (both clients). Reconnect banner below the top bar, full width, live-sync's texts. While not `live`, feature intent controls are disabled (live-sync) but window management keeps working. | |
| FR-SHELL-06 | DM top bar: campaign switcher (CAMP §7); *(proposed, OQ-SHELL-11)* presence badge "N/M online" (tooltip: names), click opens `camp.players`. Player top bar: campaign-management's wording, "Playing as **‹player›** in **‹campaign›**"; renames show ≤ 1 s. While not `live`: FR-SHELL-33. | Presence: [FR-SYNC-23](live-sync.md#6-functional-requirements) |
| FR-SHELL-07 | A window type registers: `kind` (`<prefix>.<name>`), title, clients (`dm` / `player` / both), default + min size, default position, default open, launcher icon/label/order, optional `available(store)`, component. *(Sprint 4)* Optional `restoreOpen?: false` (FR-SHELL-42). *(Sprint 5)* `defaultSize` may be `(vp) => Size` (FR-SHELL-51); optional `previousDefaults?: Size[]` (FR-SHELL-53). Only registered, available kinds for this client render. | OQ-SHELL-07 |
| FR-SHELL-08 | Open (launcher or `openWindow`): opens at the window's last geometry, else its default, or the first free slot if the default is covered by an open window (#144; built geometry in [protocol §18](../protocol/README.md#18-scenes-client-registries), which supersedes the §7 positions in feature specs); brings it to front. Already open → brought to front; minimised → restored. One instance per kind in MVP (`main`). *(Sprint 4)* "Default, or the first free slot" becomes **auto placement** (FR-SHELL-40); a **feature-driven** open whose stored rect overlaps an open window is re-placed (FR-SHELL-41); `openWindow(kind, instance?, {by?: "user" \| "feature"})`, default **`"user"`** (review A19; decided #245, OQ-SHELL-28): every click-driven open (launcher, the Not-shown badge → Scenes, token menu → sheet / Stat block, notification "Open", handout rows, sheet buttons, tray staging) keeps the user's stored spot with no change at its call site. **Feature-driven** = opens a feature makes on its own: availability appearances (shell-internal) and combat's Initiative open / restore at Start and on load (`start-end.ts` / `idleInitiativeOnLoad` pass `by: "feature"` on their manager `open`; one line each, assigned to M4, OQ-SHELL-28). *(Sprint 6, #285 → A: "keeps the user's stored spot" now holds only while that spot is not covered; a covered one is re-placed, FR-SHELL-56.)* | Amended sprint 4, 6 |
| FR-SHELL-09 | Launcher button: closed / minimised → open + front; open but behind → front; open on top → minimise. Button shows open / minimised state. | |
| FR-SHELL-10 | Move by dragging the title bar; resize by edges and corner, never below min size or beyond the viewport. Pointer-down anywhere in a window brings it to front. | |
| FR-SHELL-11 | Minimise hides the window (launcher marks it); close unmounts it. Both keep its geometry for the next open. *(N1, decided #190, OQ-SHELL-21: as built, minimise also unmounted, so the two differed only by a faint launcher line; FR-SHELL-36–38 give minimise its own meaning.)* | Amended N1 |
| FR-SHELL-12 | Clamp on restore, open and viewport resize: window ≤ viewport size; whole title bar height and ≥ 120 px of its width inside the viewport, below the top bar. Clamping is render-only: it never writes the layout; writes carry each untouched window's stored geometry. Smaller viewport than the one a window was stored at: FR-SHELL-29 first. | Amended #171 (G3) |
| FR-SHELL-13 | Pointer events reach the base layer only outside windows and chrome. Exception: an HTML5 drag that starts in a window and ends over the base layer delivers its drop to the base layer (e.g. [FR-ENT-18](entities.md#6-functional-requirements) rows). Key events whose target is inside a window, the top bar, the launcher, a dialog or any editable element never reach canvas handlers. | Ctrl-D ([FR-MAP-17](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)) |
| FR-SHELL-14 | *(proposed)* Launcher menu "Reset layout" → this client's default layout, written. *(Sprint 7: which rects and the refit, FR-SHELL-80.)* | Recovery |
| FR-SHELL-15 | The layout (every window's kind, instance, state `open` / `minimised` / `closed`, geometry, z-order) is one `localStorage` entry per (campaign, owner): key `pp.layout.v1.<campaignId>.<dm \| playerId>`, `playerId` from `you`. Written synchronously on each change: open, close, minimise, move end, resize end, bring to front. Never sent to the server. *(#171)* Each window also stores the work-area size at which **its** geometry was last set (`area {w, h}` on the WindowState: open at default / placement, move end, resize end; FR-SHELL-29); untouched windows keep their own `area`; windows without it still parse. | #121 |
| FR-SHELL-16 | The entry is applied after the first snapshot after page load and after the first snapshot after `you.campaignId` changes; no entry → default layout. Other snapshots (reconnect, resync, scene switch) never open, close or move windows. A change is written to the key of the campaign it was made in. *(Sprint 4)* On apply, a window type with `restoreOpen: false` loads **closed** whatever its stored state (FR-SHELL-42). | No jumps |
| FR-SHELL-17 | On read the entry is parsed with zod: ≤ 40 windows, unique (kind, instance), kind `^[a-z]+\.[a-z0-9-]+$` ≤ 64 chars, integer geometry within ±10 000 px. Invalid → default layout + console warning; the next change overwrites it. Storage unavailable or throwing → layout kept in memory only, console warning, no error UI. | |
| FR-SHELL-18 | Saved windows of unknown or unavailable kinds are skipped on restore without error; unknown ones are dropped at the next write, unavailable ones kept. When a kind becomes available it renders in its saved state; when it becomes unavailable it unmounts and keeps its saved state. | |
| FR-SHELL-19 | Same owner in two tabs: each restores from the entry on load; writes are last-write-wins; `storage` events are ignored, so a page never applies another page's changes live. | |
| FR-SHELL-20 | Test mode only: window type `shell.test` (both clients; shows its window key and a text input). Shown iff the client is in test mode by the same signal as live-sync's probe panel ([FR-SYNC-27](live-sync.md#6-functional-requirements)). Absent otherwise. | |
| FR-SHELL-21 | The shell registers windows whose owner specs are complete: `camp.players` (DM; campaign-management's players panel incl. online dots) and, in test mode, `sync.probes` (both; live-sync's probe panel and "drop connection" button). | |
| **Alerts** (feedback F1, #148) | | |
| FR-SHELL-22 | Client API `useShellAlert()` → `alert({id, title, detail?, sound?})` (both clients). Does: (1) a full-viewport **flash** in the overlay layer, `pointer-events: none`, two pulses over ≈ 1.2 s (≤ 3 flashes/s; #154) in a semantic accent token; `prefers-reduced-motion` → one steady 1.5 s frame glow; (2) a Toast "‹title› — ‹detail›" in the client's toast region (FR-SHELL-01); (3) the sound (FR-SHELL-24–26); (4) FR-SHELL-23. A repeat of the same `id` while its flash runs restarts it, no stacking. Shown in every connection state; never blocks input. | F1, ADR-0030 |
| FR-SHELL-23 | While the page is hidden (`document.hidden`), an alert prefixes the page title with "● ‹title› — " until the page is visible again. | Alt-tabbed players |
| FR-SHELL-24 | **Sound control** (player client): top-bar speaker Button (state on / muted / locked; muted or volume 0 shows muted before locked) with a Popover: Mute Switch, Volume Slider 0–100 (default 70, unmuted), **Test** (plays `ping`, counts as an interaction, respects mute). Stored as one `localStorage` entry per (campaign, owner) `pp.sound.v1.<campaignId>.<playerId>` = `{muted, volume}`, zod-parsed; invalid or unavailable storage → defaults, in memory, console warning (as FR-SHELL-17). Never sent. DM: none in MVP (#154). *(Sprint 6, FR-SHELL-73: the speaker Button and its Popover retire; the same controls, states, key and defaults move to the Preferences window's Sound section; the identity chip shows the muted / locked state, FR-SHELL-70.)* | ADR-0030 |
| FR-SHELL-25 | **Audio unlock** (ADR-0030): one shared Web Audio context, created on the first `pointerdown` or `keydown` anywhere on the page (not at load: Chrome warns about autoplay) (e.g. the first click after joining). Until then an alert is flash + Toast only, and the speaker shows `locked` (Tooltip "Click anywhere to enable sounds"). Muted or volume 0 → no sound. No Web Audio or a failed play → flash + Toast, one console warning. | |
| FR-SHELL-26 | Sounds come from a client sound list `{id, label, play(ctx, gain)}`; MVP ships **`ping`**, synthesised with Web Audio (two short sine tones, ≤ 400 ms; no file, no licence) (OQ-SHELL-12). Unknown id → `ping` (test mode records the sound actually played). **Design for later** (tier A): DM-added sounds = small audio assets ([assets-pipeline](assets-pipeline.md)) added as sound ids; a campaign setting picks the turn-alert sound, players may override per browser. Callers pass a sound id, so nothing changes for them. | ADR-0030 |
| FR-SHELL-27 | **Test mode** (as FR-SHELL-20): `ui-topbar` carries `data-test-last-sound="<id>:<played \| muted \| locked \| failed>:<n>"` (`n` counts alerts with a sound); `shell.test` gains an **Alert** button: `alert({id: "shell.test", title: "Test alert", sound: "ping"})`. | Walkthroughs |
| FR-SHELL-28 | **Content first at default size:** a window type's default size shows ≥ 3 rows of its primary content with all its optional chrome on. First case (#153): the DM's `dice.log` with Physical dice on (strip + roll bar) showed no entries; its build raises the DM default height or gives the log area a minimum (≥ 3 entries) and lets the roll bar scroll with the body. *(R3: numbers for `dice.log` raised by FR-SHELL-35.)* | #153 nit |
| **Playtest pass** (#171) | | |
| FR-SHELL-29 | **Fit on render** (G3; amends FR-SHELL-12). *Work area* = viewport below the top bar and above the launcher bar's top edge. Per window and **per axis**, against that window's own `area` (FR-SHELL-15): if the current work-area width < `area.w` (height < `area.h`), a window that lay wholly inside its stored work area on that axis renders wholly inside the current one: a window that touched (≤ 8 px) the stored right (bottom) edge keeps that gap to the current edge; any other moves left (up) only as far as needed; then it shrinks on that axis, never below min size. If min size still doesn't fit, FR-SHELL-12 applies. A window stored partly outside the work area (parked by the user) gets FR-SHELL-12 only. Render-only, like FR-SHELL-12: nothing written; back at the stored size the window is at its stored geometry. Windows without `area` (stored before this pass): FR-SHELL-12 only until their geometry is next set. Default layouts are built for the current work area (placement, protocol §18). | G3; OQ-SHELL-17 (decided #172) |
| FR-SHELL-30 | **Tooltips** (P1): every hover hint in both clients is the shared `Tooltip` (`@ui/components/ui/overlays`) under one provider per client with **one open delay, 400 ms** (a single exported constant; (decided #172) skip delay 300 ms: moving straight from one trigger to the next opens at once). Native `title` is not used on interactive elements (it can't be delayed or suppressed); it stays only on non-interactive truncated text. Tooltip content carries `data-testid="ui-tooltip"`. | P1 |
| FR-SHELL-31 | **Tooltip vs overlay** (P1): pressing a trigger (pointer down, Enter, Space) closes its tooltip and cancels a pending open; it stays closed until the pointer leaves the trigger. While a popover, dialog, dropdown or context menu opened from a trigger is open, that trigger's tooltip never opens; focus returned to the trigger when the overlay closes doesn't open it either. Tooltips inside the open overlay work normally. | P1: Roll initiative → popover |

| FR-SHELL-34 | **Free area** (R1; decided #178, [OQ-MAP-33](scenes-map.md#15-open-questions)): pure `freeArea(rects, vp)` + hook `useFreeArea()` in a new `free-area.ts`. Free area = the largest axis-aligned rectangle inside the work area (FR-SHELL-29), right of the tool rail (x ≥ 68 px), that no **rendered open** window overlaps (minimised / closed windows, toasts, popovers and the attack popup don't count). Ties → the higher, then the more left one. Smaller than 320×240 or than 25 % of the work area → the work area right of the rail. The hook also returns a counter of **appearances**: windows rendered because their kind became available while open (FR-SHELL-18; e.g. player `cbt.initiative` on Start), not user opens. Kinds that first become available in a **reconnect snapshot** (e.g. a player offline at Start) count as **one** appearance for that snapshot. `noteAppearance()` (same file) adds one for feature-driven opens: [FR-CBT-60](combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements) after the DM's own Start. Read-only otherwise; nothing stored or sent. Consumer: [FR-MAP-44](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements). *(Sprint 4)* The candidates behind this choice are exposed by FR-SHELL-44; the camera picks among them (FR-MAP-58). | R1 |

| FR-SHELL-36 | **Minimised = parked** (amends FR-SHELL-11, §12): a minimised window stays **mounted** but not rendered (`hidden`): its component keeps its state (scroll position, tab, unsaved field drafts, open sections); it takes no pointer or key events, is not focusable and doesn't count for the free area (FR-SHELL-34, unchanged). Restore shows it as it was. **Close** unmounts: the next open starts fresh (geometry kept, FR-SHELL-11). Same for DM and players. Unavailable kinds (FR-SHELL-18) unmount whatever their state. After a reload a minimised window is mounted fresh, still minimised. | N1 |
| FR-SHELL-37 | **Launcher states look different** (amends FR-SHELL-09's "shows open / minimised state"): **open** = pressed background + solid bar under the label (as built); **minimised** = no pressed background, no bar, a small hollow ring under the label; **closed** = nothing. Tooltip "‹Label› (open)" / "‹Label› (minimised — click to restore)" / "‹Label›". Closed windows never show it. A throwing `activity` is logged once and treated as `null`. Badges (`badge`, e.g. the bell's unread count) are unchanged and independent. First opt-ins: `dice.log` ([FR-DICE-33](dice-roll-log.md#6-functional-requirements)); `cbt.initiative` with `activity` = `round:turnCombatantId` of the started encounter (settled S2, OQ-SHELL-22; the line is combat-tracker's). | N1 |
| FR-SHELL-39 | **Grow to content** (G4 for combat-tracker; settled S2, OQ-CBT-52; independent of N1): a window type may declare `growToContent?: true` (FR-SHELL-07). While the window has not been resized by the user, its **rendered** height = max(stored h, content height), capped so the bottom stays inside the work area (FR-SHELL-29); beyond the cap the body scrolls. It grows downward from its top (a bottom-anchored window keeps its bottom and grows up). Render-only like FR-SHELL-29: growth is never written. A user **resize end** writes `h` as today and sets `fixedH: true` on its WindowState: from then on the stored height wins (no growth). "Reset layout" clears it. **Rendered rects** (review P2b-8): `renderedWindowRects` (`free-area.ts`) takes each grown window's **measured** rendered height (reported by `ShellWindow` on content resize), not the stored `h`; every consumer reads it from there: the free area (FR-SHELL-34), the DM toast keep-out ([FR-NOTE-32](notifications.md#6-functional-requirements)) and the attack popup placement ("left of Initiative", [FR-CBT-55/56](combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements)). The map never refits on growth (FR-MAP-44). First opt-in: `cbt.initiative` (combat-tracker FR-CBT-77). *(Sprint 5)* The cap is also the next window or keep-out below it (FR-SHELL-52). | G4 seam |

| FR-SHELL-40 | **Auto placement, token-aware** (decided #245, OQ-SHELL-24): applies to every window that opens **without the user choosing a spot**: the default layout (first load, Reset layout), feature-driven opens and restores (`openWindow` with `by: "feature"`, e.g. Initiative at Start, [FR-CBT-60](combat-tracker.md#6-functional-requirements)), availability appearances (FR-SHELL-34), and launcher opens with no stored geometry. Candidates = today's placement slots (the fitted default rect; full-size free slots at either edge column, then the tallest ≥ min height; protocol §18), each **scored by the on-screen area of this client's token boxes it would cover** (`tokenScreenBoxes`: the tokens drawn now, current camera; DM incl. hidden, players their store only). Least covered wins; ties → today's order (default column, nearest). New: the default rect is scored too, not only when another window is in the way. **Toast keep-out** (*seams S7, review A11*): a candidate overlapping this client's keep-out box is dropped while any candidate clear of it exists. The box is **exported by `toast-region.ts`** (`toastKeepOut(client, vp) → Rect`; players bottom-right above the launcher, DM top-centre below the top bar), sized for **3 toasts + D's roll-popup stack** (DICEX §7: a ≈ 280-px card with a 260 × 96 picture-in-picture, header and total); M4 creates the export with those sizes, **D7 owns the constants afterwards** and grows them without touching placement; with none, the least overlap wins after token cover. | FR-SHELL-55 | **Tool bars push windows right** (decided #256, the DM's addition to OQ-SHELL-38 A; e.g. the player's Initiative opens top-left, so the AoE panel opened under it couldn't be seen): when a map tool's bar / panel appears at its home spot top-left ([FR-MAP-60](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements): AoE panel, fog bar, structure / props panels, the DM ruler's Diagonals) or changes size, every rendered open window whose rect overlaps the bar's rect grown by 12 px is **pushed right**, in x order: x = the bar's right edge + 12; a pushed rect that lands on another open window moves on past it (its right edge + 12), repeatedly; it keeps its size if it fits before vw − 12, else shrinks its width toward its min; if even the min width doesn't fit, a launcher-opened window is auto-minimised (FR-SHELL-47) and a protected one stays at the right edge at min width (it may then overlap). Pushes apply to protected windows too (moved, never covered). Same for both clients. *(Sprint 7: "not restored" is retired if OQ-SHELL-54 A: FR-SHELL-81; the DM stack keeps clear of a pushed window, FR-NOTE-44 (FR-SHELL-82 retired).)* | #256; s7 |

| FR-SHELL-56 | **Reopen rule** (decided #285 → A; BL-38; amends FR-SHELL-08, -41, -47): applies to every **user** open (`by: "user"`, the default: launcher button, "⋯" menu item, token menu → sheet, notification "Open", sheet buttons, tray staging) of a window whose state is `closed` or `minimised` (a restore). Bringing an already open window to front never moves it. **(1)** Its **shown rect** = the stored geometry as it would render now (FR-SHELL-29 fit, FR-SHELL-12 clamp, FR-SHELL-39 / -52 grown height). **(2)** **Covered** (proposed, OQ-SHELL-41 A) = the shown rect overlaps (≥ 1 px, no gap needed) another rendered open window or a **hard** keep-out region (FR-SHELL-48: the players' toast corner, the active tool bar); the DM's soft top-centre box and tokens don't count. **(3)** Not covered → the stored rect, `data-placed="stored"` (the user's spot, as today). **(4)** Covered → auto placement exactly as a first open (FR-SHELL-45 with -57 / -58, -46, -47: may auto-minimise the least recently used other user-opened window), the new rect **written** with `area` (FR-SHELL-15), `data-placed="reopened"`; the old spot is not kept (the next reopen starts from the new one). **(5)** `fixedDefault` types: unchanged. **Protected windows** (this client's `defaultOpen` types: Game log, DM Initiative, the player's sheet and Initiative; FR-SHELL-45) **keep their stored rect** on a covered reopen, so they never lose their zone (rulings-b #5); instead every other open window overlapping that rect is re-placed by auto placement as if just opened (FR-SHELL-45 with the reopened protected window as an obstacle), in z-order from the lowest, and one that finds no clear spot is auto-minimised (FR-SHELL-47); each moved one gets `data-placed="auto"` (written). **(6)** Not a user open, so unchanged: applying the layout on load (FR-SHELL-16: a layout stored on a bigger screen may still render overlapping, render-only, until a window is reopened or Reset layout), snapshots, viewport resizes (FR-SHELL-29). User moves and resizes may still overlap anything (FR-SHELL-48). Pure in `manager.ts` (`open`), same inputs as FR-SHELL-41. Same for both clients. *(Sprint 7: (2) "covered" stays OQ-SHELL-41 A (windows and hard regions) for a spot **the user set** (move / resize end); proposed, OQ-SHELL-55 **B**: a stored spot the shell chose (auto-placed, pushed, returned, default) also counts as covered when its shown rect covers a PC footprint + 4 px (sim-10 b02: Party's auto-placed spot, fitted to 1280×720, over Brakka 100 % and Mira 47 %); the WindowState gains optional `userSpot: true`, set at a move / resize end and cleared by any shell placement; (4)'s placement and the protected-reopen moves in (5) use FR-SHELL-77, so a re-placed window auto-minimises the next launcher window rather than land on a PC.)* | sim-9 a (a04, a06); #285; s7 |
| FR-SHELL-57 | **PC rule on footprints** (BL-39; amends FR-SHELL-45 (2), FR-SHELL-46's gate): a PC (token with an owner, this client's store) counts as covered when the candidate overlaps its **footprint**: its grid cells on screen at the current camera, for **every size** (tiny 1 × 1 cell as drawn ([FR-MAP-14](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements); integrator, WF1 build), small and medium 1 × 1, large 2 × 2, huge 3 × 3, gargantuan 4 × 4; not the `map-token` node's client rect, which can include the name label and badges), grown by **4 px** per side (the margin is proposed, OQ-SHELL-42 A). `tokenScreenBoxes` (`token-boxes.ts`) **computes** the footprint as the `map-token` group's absolute position + its size's cells × the on-screen cell px (stage scale × grid cell size), with the size read from the token record in the store by the node's id (rulings-b #17: no child-index read, so CP5's later `TokensLayer.tsx` edits can't break it; no canvas file changes); the rank test changes from centre-inside to box overlap. The **shrink to clear PCs** (`clearOfPcs`) cuts a candidate so it ends **12 px** (`EDGE_GAP`) before the nearest grown PC box on that side (narrower keeping its left or right edge, shorter keeping its top or bottom), never at a centre; a cut below min size is dropped. Applies to every auto placement, re-placements (FR-SHELL-41, -56) and FR-SHELL-47's re-runs. `ui-window` of an auto-placed window gains `data-pc-clear="true"` when no PC footprint is under it. | sim-9 a (a01, a05) |
| FR-SHELL-58 | **A spot over no token first** (BL-39): new rank **(2c)**, after (2b) and before (3): the candidate overlaps **no token footprint** (every token this client draws, DM incl. hidden; boxes as FR-SHELL-57, no 4 px). So a clear inward slot over no token beats a clear (or shrunk) edge slot over tokens (sim-9 a01: Compendium at 164,172 over 6 tokens while 576,60 was clear and empty). FR-SHELL-46 adds inward columns whenever no edge candidate passes (1), (1b), (2) and (2c). When every clear candidate covers a token, today's order follows: (3) edge before inward, (4) soft regions, (5) least token area. Token-free input (first load, Reset layout before tokens are drawn) → (2c) holds everywhere, so §7's default layouts are unchanged. **Candidates from tokens** (rulings-b #1): every drawn token footprint's edges also give rows and columns (± 12, like windows'): x = its right edge + 12 and its left edge − 12 − w, y = its bottom + 12 and its top − 12 − h, for ≤ **200** boxes, so a token-free gap between token clusters is found even when no window edge lines up with it. **Spot cap:** ≤ **4,000** candidate spots per open; past it, token-derived columns and rows farthest (Manhattan) from the fitted default rect are dropped first. **Intent** (rulings-b #16): token-free beats size and the soft box on purpose: a min-size spot over no token beats a full-size spot over one goblin, and a token-free spot inside the DM's soft top-centre box beats a full-size spot over tokens (the toasts sit above windows anyway, FR-NOTE-42); both are named test cases. | sim-9 a01 |

The fix keys on `:focus-visible`, not on the pointer alone. | BL-37; #199; FR-FOG-17 |

| FR-SHELL-88 | **Test hooks** (walkthroughs): `ui-window` `data-auto-minimised-for="<kind>"` while auto-minimised by FR-SHELL-77 or a screen-full re-run (cleared on restore); `data-placed` gains `returned` (FR-SHELL-81); `ui-play-page` `data-auto-minimise-log` = the last placement's moves "kind>kind;…" (client memory, test mode only). | — |

**FR-SHELL-77 steps** (for a window `W`; "auto-minimisable" = rendered, open, type neither `defaultOpen` nor `fixedDefault`, not `W`):

- **(a) Rank** every candidate as today (FR-SHELL-45 with -46, -57, -58), at the full size and at the useful size (FR-SHELL-79).
- **(b) Accept** the winner iff it is clear of every rendered open window and hard keep-out (rank (1)), covers no PC footprint + 4 px (rank (2), FR-SHELL-57) and is at least `W`'s useful size.
- **(c) Look ahead, then auto-minimise:** rank once more with **every** auto-minimisable window removed. No accepted spot even then → go to (d) and auto-minimise nothing. Else auto-minimise the first window in FR-SHELL-78's order (as by its title bar, FR-SHELL-36; written; `data-auto-minimised="true"`, `data-auto-minimised-for="<W's kind>"`) and go back to (a). Each window at most once per placement, so the loop runs at most as many times as there are auto-minimisable windows (≤ 40, FR-SHELL-17).
- **(d) Shrink:** nothing accepted after (c) → rank with every shrink down to `W`'s min size allowed (FR-SHELL-45 (6), `clearOfPcs`); accept the winner iff (b) holds without the useful-size clause. *(Integrator #319, FU1 walkthrough U1: when no min-size spot is PC-clear either, the same look-ahead runs at min size: auto-minimise windows in FR-SHELL-78's order if that yields a PC-clear min-size spot; e.g. DM 1280×720, Encounters opens 368×320 with the previous launcher window auto-minimised.)*
- **(e) Last resort:** the clear-of-windows candidate (else FR-SHELL-45's protected last resort) with the **least PC footprint area**, then the least token area, then today's order; `data-pc-clear` absent.
- **Protected `W`** (DM Initiative at Start, the player's Initiative appearance, a protected reopen): (a)–(e) auto-minimise launcher windows for it; a protected window is never auto-minimised; a PC it still covers is the map's to clear (FR-MAP-44 (b), FR-MAP-90).

| **Sprint 7, track ST** (stats) | | |
| FR-SHELL-93 | **Window type `stats.board`** "Stats" ([stats](stats.md) FR-STATS-18, -19), registered per **FR-SHELL-86** (seam S5): one `defineWindow` line in each client's `windows.ts` (append); `defaultOpen: false` (parkable, never protected); no keep-out; **default 400 × 412**, `top-left`, `dx` 56; **min 320 × 280** (FU's limit); body scrolls. Launcher: lucide `Trophy`, label "Stats", order right after Party; no `activity` (stats aren't in the store). An ordinary launcher window for auto placement, the reopen rule, screen-full auto-minimise and #310 parking. ST2 appends its **golden row** and **size-table row** (FR-SHELL-86's tables) in its own branch. | OQ-STATS-17 resolved (S5) |
| FR-SHELL-94 | **Feature open on awards** (FR-STATS-20). On a **live** `stats-awards` notification (never from a snapshot), the client calls `openStatsAwards(fromN)` (`@ui/features/stats/open.ts`, the `openPartyTab` pattern, [protocol §93](../protocol/README.md#93-sprint-5-loot-tab-p3)). It stores the view `{tab: "session", fromN}` for the viewer's key, then `openWindow("stats.board", main, {by: "feature"})`. A closed window is auto-placed (FR-SHELL-40 / -45, may park per FU). A minimised one is restored. An open one is fronted and switched. A player offline at the time uses the notification's **Open** (FR-NOTE-48), which does the same as a user open. | |

**Sprint 8, track WF** (#333; picks decided at DM gate 1, #335)

**The rule in plain words** (FR-SHELL-97). When a window must be placed, the shell tries, in order:

1. its full size, somewhere that covers no window, tool bar or player character;
2. smaller, down to its useful size (and the Game log / Initiative / sheet squeezed down to theirs, OQ-SHELL-59);
3. for players: let the map zoom out a little so the PCs come clear (OQ-SHELL-64);
4. put away the window you used longest ago, then try again from 1;
5. below its useful size, down to its minimum;
6. last resort: cover as little of a PC as possible (flagged, tested never to happen on the reference sessions).

| ID | Requirement | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| FR-SHELL-95 | **One placer.** A pure, deterministic `settle(world, trigger) → Plan` (`play-shell/placer.ts`) is the only writer of window geometry and auto-minimising, besides a user's own move / resize. **Triggers:** `load` (layout applied; it includes FR-SHELL-53's default-size migration, and runs **once more after the first token draw** so PCs count), `open`, `reopen`, `appear` (incl. Initiative at Start), `settle` (grown height), `viewport`, `push`, `return`, `drag-end`, `reset`. **`viewport`:** while the viewport changes, windows only render-fit (FR-SHELL-29); one settle runs, and writes, 150 ms after the last resize event (integrator, WF1: so it always lands before the map's 200 ms resize refit, which follows it). Close / minimise move nothing. `placeWindow` stays as the spot search. `autoPlace`, `pushWindows`, `belowBar`, `followDefaults` and `clearAround` stop being entry points. | #333; rulings-b 7, 13 |
| FR-SHELL-96 | **World.** Viewport, client, windows (state, rect, `area`, `z`, `userSpot`, `userSize`, def), keep-outs (incl. The canvas provides a **base port** `{pcs, tokens, dryFit}`; the shell never imports it. | #318 |
| FR-SHELL-97 | **The ladder** (#333). The first rung with an accepted spot wins. Accepted = clear of windows and hard keep-outs (FR-SHELL-98) and of PC footprints. **L1** target size. **L2** down to the useful size, then the protected squeeze (FR-SHELL-101). **L2b** the map yields (FR-SHELL-108). **L3** look-ahead with every launcher window removed: if that helps, put away the LRU launcher window (FR-SHELL-78) and restart. **L4** below useful to min, same look-ahead (#319). **L5** least PC area, flagged (`data-pc-clear` absent). Rank within a rung as built (FR-SHELL-45, -57, -58). A protected window is never put away. | BL-64 |

Else (a `moved` camera, or a PC under the zone) it goes through the ladder, with the zone as its first candidate. This applies on `load`, `appear`, `viewport`, its own `reopen` and `reset`; `data-placed="zone"`. Launcher windows in a zone, **user spots included**, are displaced (cause `window:<kind>`) and return when the cause ends (End, or the protected window closes). The memory is dropped on a user move / resize / close / minimise, reload, reset. **The cause ends** when the keep-out goes, when the viewport is **≥ the recorded size on both axes**, or when the kind closes / ends. Then the window returns, ascending z, iff untouched and the old rect is clear (a shell spot also PC-clear; user spots ignore PCs).
