**Sprint 9, track PM: window memory** (DM #338, decided; amends ADR-0060 per the [proposed ADR](../architecture/adr/proposed-s9-pm-window-memory.md); picks marked *proposed* wait for the DM)

**The rule in plain words.** A window you moved or resized is yours: it opens exactly where and how big you left it, on top, and nothing else moves for it. On a smaller screen it is only nudged back on-screen. A tool bar can push it aside while the tool is open; it comes back to the same spot after. Windows you never touched are still placed by the ladder (FR-SHELL-97). Reset layout makes every window untouched again.

**Reopen algorithm** (FR-SHELL-146–150; pure, in `settle`). Inputs: the stored `WindowState` `w` (rect, `area`, `z`, `userSpot`, `userSize`), the viewport `vp`, the hard keep-outs shown now `K`, the windows `ws`.

1. `remembered(w)` = `w.userSpot || w.userSize` (FR-SHELL-144). Not remembered → the ladder as built (FR-SHELL-151).
2. Stored rect `s` = `w`'s rect, unchanged. Nothing else in `ws` changes; no put-away; no displacement-memory entry for any other window; `plan.yields = false` (FR-SHELL-149).
3. Render `r = renderedRect(s, w.area, def.minSize, vp)`: FR-SHELL-29's fit (move back inside the work area first, keeping a far-edge gap ≤ 20 px; shrink only what still doesn't fit, never below min), then FR-SHELL-12's clamp and the rail inset (FR-SHELL-109). Render-only: `s` is never rewritten (FR-SHELL-147).
4. `r` overlaps a shown hard keep-out `k ∈ K` (a tool bar, a chrome keep-out; not the toast box) → the push of FR-SHELL-150; else `data-placed="stored"`.
5. z: `w.z = maxZ(ws) + 1`, then `normaliseZ` (FR-SHELL-148).

| ID | Requirement | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| FR-SHELL-144 | **Remembered window** (proposed, OQ-SHELL-74 **A**). A window is *remembered* iff its stored entry has `userSpot` (set at a user move end) or `userSize` (a user resize end); else *untouched*. A click, focus, scroll, tab switch, minimise or close makes nothing remembered. The flags are cleared only by Reset layout (FR-SHELL-153); no placer write drops them any more (today `shellRect` drops them: it now runs for untouched windows only). | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-145 | **The placer leaves remembered windows alone.** No trigger (`load`, `open`, `reopen`, `appear`, `settle`, `viewport`, `return`, `drag-end`) moves, resizes, squeezes (FR-SHELL-101), displaces (FR-SHELL-99, -102) or puts away (FR-SHELL-97 L3) a remembered window, a protected one included. The only exceptions: a tool-bar / chrome keep-out push (FR-SHELL-150) and Reset (FR-SHELL-153). `settle` stays the only writer (FR-SHELL-95). | #338; amends FR-SHELL-97, -99, -101–104 |
| FR-SHELL-146 | **Reopen exactly.** Every way a remembered window comes up (launcher open or toggle, restore from minimised, `openWindow(…, {by: "feature"})`, `appear` incl. Initiative at Start, a reload's `load` settle, a shortcut / palette command) uses the reopen algorithm above: its stored rect and size, even over other windows, protected zones, the soft DM toast box or PCs. FR-SHELL-56's "covered" test and FR-SHELL-41's "feature opens never land on a window" apply to untouched windows only. `data-placed="stored"`. | #338; amends FR-SHELL-08, -41, -56, -99 (5), -107 |
| FR-SHELL-147 | **Smaller screen: nudge, don't re-place** (proposed, OQ-SHELL-75 **A**). A remembered window keeps its stored rect on every viewport change; it renders per step 3 (moved back on-screen, shrunk only where it is bigger than the work area, never below min). Nothing is written, so a return to the bigger screen shows the stored rect exactly. A window the user left partly outside its work area (parked, FR-SHELL-12) keeps FR-SHELL-12's clamp only (title bar + 120 px visible). A user move / resize of the nudged window writes the new rect (it is theirs). | #338; FR-SHELL-29, -103 |
| FR-SHELL-148 | **Most recent on top.** A window goes to the top of the z-order (`maxZ + 1`, `normaliseZ`) when it is opened in any way (FR-SHELL-146's list, untouched windows too), on a pointer-down inside it (FR-SHELL-10, as built) and at a move / resize start. **New:** `appear` and a feature open of a closed or minimised window front it too (today `appear` keeps the stored z). A placer write (placed, pushed, returned, grown, squeezed, put away) never changes z. `load` keeps the stored z. So FR-SHELL-78's put-away order (ascending z = least recently used) is unchanged. | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-149 | **Nothing makes room for a remembered window.** Its open runs no ladder for anyone: no other window is moved, shrunk, squeezed or put away; no displacement memory changes; the map gets no `window` refit and does not yield (FR-SHELL-108, [FR-MAP-115](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)). Windows under it stay under it. | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-150 | **Tool-bar pushes stay temporary** (#338; proposed, OQ-SHELL-76 **A**). A shown hard keep-out (the active tool bar, a chrome keep-out, FR-SHELL-113) that overlaps a remembered window's rendered rect + 12 px pushes it by the **least move** that clears the bar + 12: below the bar (`y = bar.bottom + 12`) or right of it (`x = bar.right + 12`), whichever moves the rect less (ties: below), same size, clamped on-screen; other windows and PCs are ignored, nothing is shrunk or put away. It keeps displacement memory `toolbar:<id>` with its `userSpot` / `userSize`. When the bar goes (FR-SHELL-102's cause end) it goes back to exactly its stored rect, **with no clear-spot test** (it may land over a window opened meanwhile; z unchanged, FR-SHELL-148). A user move / resize while pushed makes the new rect the remembered one (memory dropped, as built). Untouched windows: FR-SHELL-104 unchanged. `data-placed="pushed"` / `"returned"`. | #338; amends FR-SHELL-102, -104, -105 |
| FR-SHELL-151 | **Untouched windows keep the ladder** (proposed, OQ-SHELL-77 **A**, -79 **A**). An untouched window is placed exactly as FR-SHELL-95–111 on every trigger, with every remembered window as a **fixed obstacle**: rank (1) "clear" counts it, the look-ahead never removes it, L3 puts away only untouched launcher windows (FR-SHELL-78's order among them), L2's squeeze only untouched protected windows. With no accepted spot: L4, L5 as built, then FR-SHELL-45's last resort, which may overlap a remembered window (the new one on top, FR-SHELL-148). **Zones** (FR-SHELL-99): an untouched protected window still takes its zone when PC-clear; untouched launcher windows in it are displaced and return as built; a remembered window in it **stays** (under the protected window until clicked). The first open of a window is always untouched, so it is still placed PC-clear (#333). | #338; amends FR-SHELL-97, -99, -101 |
| FR-SHELL-152 | **A put-away window stays put away** (BL-85 superseded, #338). An untouched window that L3 put away for another window (`cause=open:<kind>`) stays minimised when that window closes; its launcher ring shows it and one click reopens it by the reopen rule. Transient causes keep their returns (viewport, tool bar, zones: FR-SHELL-102, -103). Reason: with window memory the placer only ever puts away windows it placed itself; popping them back was rejected at #335 (OQ-SHELL-60 C). | BL-85 |
| FR-SHELL-153 | **Reset layout** (amends FR-SHELL-80): clears `userSpot`, `userSize` and `fixedH` on **every** entry (closed ones too), drops displacement memory, writes the named default layout (zones, token-free), closes launcher windows, then one Fit (as built). Every window is untouched after it, so its next open is placed by the ladder. Both clients; from the launcher "⋯", Preferences → Layout, or SP's palette command (OQ-SHELL-80). | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-154 | **Old entries.** No new stored field; the layout key and shape stay (`pp.layout.v1`, FR-SHELL-17). Entries stored before sprint 9 keep their flags: a window with `userSpot` / `userSize` (incl. FR-SHELL-100's inferred `userSize`) is remembered from the first load. | 0 B |
| FR-SHELL-155 | **A window type may absorb a removed one** (registry, FR-SHELL-07): `absorbs?: readonly string[]` (kinds no longer registered) and `onAbsorb?(fromKind, you)`. On `load` (FR-SHELL-16), for each stored entry of an absorbed kind in state `open` / `minimised` while the absorbing kind's entry is `closed` or missing: the absorbing window takes that `state` and `z`; its geometry is its own stored rect if remembered, else the `load` settle places it as untouched (the absorbed rect is never reused: a different size). Then `onAbsorb` runs once (client memory; e.g. party-board picks its tab). The absorbed entry is dropped at that write (FR-SHELL-18) and the entry written (`migrated`). Both open → the absorbing window keeps its own state; the absorbed entry is just dropped. | migrates `party.summary` |
| FR-SHELL-156 | **Party Manager row** ([FR-PARTY-59](party-board.md#611-sprint-9-party-manager-track-pm)): `party.board` title and launcher label **"Party Manager"** (icon, order 58, kind, test IDs unchanged), `absorbs: ["party.summary"]`. Size-table row, both clients, all four sizes: **default 550 × 560** (#338: 1.25 × 440; capped to the client's band, e.g. 550 × 412 at 1280×720), **useful 440 × 400** (proposed, OQ-SHELL-78 **A**; criterion: the Party status tab's 6 PC rows whole on `cb-party-six`, and the labels Party status, Handouts, Inventory, Loot whole), **min 340 × 360**, `minOpenH` 400 (unchanged). At 1280×720 the default layout leaves 788 × 412 left of the DM's Initiative (550 fits) and 576 × 412 between a player's Initiative and sheet (550 fits); at 1440×900 the DM's Compendium (440) and Party Manager no longer fit side by side at full width (948 px), so the ladder opens the second at ≥ 440 (L2) or puts the first away (L3). | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-157 | **`party.summary` removed**: its `defineWindow`, its line in each `windows.ts` (the owner deletes its own line, the `loot.log` precedent, seams S4 2026-10-17), its size-table row, its harness and golden rows, and its launcher button (both clients). Stored entries: FR-SHELL-155. FR-SHELL-140–143 retired (IDs kept). | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-158 | **Placer bench** (BL-82). FR-SHELL-119's gates stay (settle ≤ 25 ms best of 5 for 12 windows × 200 boxes; ≤ 100 ms at 40), now **inside the full `bun run bench`**. Fix (root cause by profile first, printed in the bench output): **(1)** the L3 look-ahead runs once per placed window, not once per restart (its question, "is there an accepted spot with every put-away-able window removed?", has the same answer after each put-away, because the removed set is the same windows); **(2)** one candidate grid and PC-box list per (window, size, gap) per settle, reused by the 12-px / 4-px retries and the look-ahead; **(3)** remembered windows skip the ladder (FR-SHELL-145). Target ≤ 20 ms alone. A builder who can't reach 25 ms in the full run reports the profile to the orchestrator; no re-budget in the branch. | BL-82 |
| FR-SHELL-159 | **A squeezed protected window grows back** (BL-87; sim-12 glitch 5: DM 1440×900, Party status or a sheet opened → Game log 1150×240 → 460×160 for good). Root cause first (suspects: `growBack` loops launcher windows only; the squeezed window's return to its zone runs the zone ladder when the zone covers a PC at the current camera, which accepts the squeezed size at L2). Fix: **(1)** when the cause (`window:<kind>`) ends (the kind closes, is minimised or is put away), an untouched protected window that was squeezed goes back to its pre-squeeze rect iff clear of windows and hard keep-outs; with a `fitted` camera a PC under it is the map's to clear (the plan asks for a `window` refit, [FR-MAP-114](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)); with `moved`, the zone ladder as built; **(2)** `growBack` (FR-SHELL-103 up, `return`) also grows shell-shrunk untouched protected windows toward their zone rect (L1–L2 in the zone, nothing put away). A remembered protected window is never squeezed (FR-SHELL-145). Outcome: open Party Manager or a sheet at DM 1440×900, close it → Game log 1150 × 240 after that settle. | BL-87 |
| FR-SHELL-160 | **Harness sets** (BL-80, first half). `party.summary` leaves D3, P1, P3 (the 10 BL-80 cells go with it); `party.board` (550, Party status tab) takes its place in **P1 and P3** (players who watched Party status now open Party Manager) and stays in D1 and P2. PM1's first commit reruns the harness and regenerates `replay.failing.json` (never hand-edited); a slice flips only its own cells; a cell that can't be PC-clear at 1280×720 with Party Manager at its useful size is named as an L5 exemption with a reason (FR-SHELL-116; the list may only shrink). | BL-80 |
| FR-SHELL-161 | **Launcher labels** (BL-80, second half: "full labels only from 1920 px"): **reasoned drop, no behaviour change.** Labels show only when every button's label fits (FR-SHELL-43, #245 OQ-SHELL-25 A); below that, icons with Tooltips, then "⋯". Removing the Party status button lowers the threshold, "Party Manager" raises it; `launcher-fit.test.ts` records the measured threshold per client as a number (not a promise), and SP's command palette names every window (OQ-SHELL-80). | BL-80 |
| FR-SHELL-162 | **Harness under window memory** (extends FR-SHELL-115, -116). New path **`memory`** per set × camera × size: **(1)** drag each open launcher window to a scripted spot (one over a PC, one over another window, one at the right edge, one partly off-screen) and resize one; **(2)** close + reopen each; **(3)** reload; **(4)** resize down the chain and back; **(5)** push + return; **(6)** Start; **(7)** Reset; **(8)** open one untouched window. Invariants: I1 (PCs), I2 (overlap) and I3 (useful) **exempt remembered windows** (they may sit over PCs and windows, at any size ≥ min); I2 still holds between untouched windows and keep-outs, and an untouched window lands on a remembered one only at FR-SHELL-45's last resort; I4 = only untouched launcher windows are put away, LRU first. New: **I9** a remembered window's stored rect is unchanged by every step except a push (and is back after the return) and Reset; **I10** its rendered rect = step 3 of the reopen algorithm; at the size it was set, rendered = stored; **I11** after every open / focus / drag the touched window has the highest z; **I12** no remembered window is ever put away, squeezed or displaced; **I13** after Reset no entry has `userSpot` / `userSize` / `fixedH` and the layout = the named default. Named `FR-SHELL-115 replay <set> <camera> @<size> memory`. | #338 |
| FR-SHELL-163 | **Goldens** (FR-SHELL-117): regenerated once in PM1 (`UPDATE_GOLDEN=1`, diff reviewed), PM2 regenerates for its rows after PM1 merges. New golden table **`memory`**: the scripted spots of FR-SHELL-162 (1) → exact stored and rendered rects at the four sizes, both clients. `layout-checks.ts` (the brute oracle) learns the remembered exemptions. | — |
| FR-SHELL-164 | **Test hooks**: `ui-window` `data-remembered="true"` while remembered; `data-placed="stored"` on an exact reopen; test mode `data-z` (the z rank, 1 = bottom); `data-settle-log` entries `<trigger>:<kind>>remembered`. | — |

- **Retired / amended by sprint 9** (IDs kept): FR-SHELL-140–143 retired (`party.summary` gone, FR-SHELL-157); FR-SHELL-56 (2)'s "covered" re-place, -41 and -106 → untouched windows only (FR-SHELL-146 covers remembered ones; -106's "kept as a user spot" is now the general rule); FR-SHELL-99's "launcher windows in a zone, **user spots included**, are displaced" → untouched only (FR-SHELL-151); FR-SHELL-101 squeezes untouched protected windows only (today: not `userSize`; now not `userSpot` either); FR-SHELL-102's return test ("iff untouched and the old rect is clear") → remembered windows return without the clear test (FR-SHELL-150); FR-SHELL-104 → untouched windows (remembered: FR-SHELL-150); FR-SHELL-80 → FR-SHELL-153. Unchanged: FR-SHELL-95 (one writer), -96, -98, -100, -103 for untouched windows, -105, -107–117 except as listed, -119 (gates).
- **DM view / player view:** the same rules in both clients, each on its own layout entry, PCs and zones. The DM's remembered windows never move at Start; a player's remembered sheet or Initiative stays where the player put it.
- **Seams** (registration for sprint 9's other tracks, no frame edit by them): RS's Stronghold window and Party Manager tabs, SP's palette and shortcuts, FX's toast runtime: OQ-SHELL-80–82 and §14.

## 7. UI/UX notes

Per [visual-direction](../design/visual-direction.md): shadcn defaults, dark only, laptop viewports (≥ 1280 px), semantic tokens only, map is the hero (thin chrome over the canvas, no reserved margins).

- **Top bar** (thin, overlays the canvas): left = campaign (DM: switcher Select + "New campaign" Dialog; player: "Playing as …"); right = DM presence Badge, player speaker Button (lucide volume / volume-x icon, FR-SHELL-24), connection indicator (Badge + Tooltip).
- **Alert flash** (FR-SHELL-22): overlay layer above dialogs, inset frame + translucent fill in an accent placeholder token (theme-system names it); Toast in the existing region. **Tool rail**: left edge, below the top bar; slot filled by scenes-map, empty otherwise.
- **Tooltips** (FR-SHELL-30/31): shadcn Tooltip only, small panel surface, side top by default; same delay everywhere; e.g. the sheet's "Shift: advantage · Ctrl: disadvantage" hint becomes a Tooltip and stays shut while the roll popover is open.
- **Launcher**: bottom-centre bar of icon Buttons with Tooltips (BG2 "bottom action bar" motif later via theme); trailing "⋯" menu with "Reset layout". **Toasts**: one region per client: player bottom-right; DM top-centre below the top bar (FR-SHELL-01; unchanged in sprint 5, #256).
- **Window**: panel surface; title bar (title, minimise, close), scrolling body, resize handle; focused window uses a semantic border token (names: [theme-system](post-mvp/theme-system.md)).
- **Minimised (N1, decided #190, OQ-SHELL-21):** launcher button: open = pressed + bar, minimised = hollow ring, closed = plain; the "new" dot sits top-left of the button (the badge keeps top-right), accent token, no number. Title-bar Tooltips say what each button does (FR-SHELL-37).
- **Sprint 4:** launcher on a narrow viewport: icon-only buttons with Tooltips, then the "⋯" DropdownMenu lists the overflowed kinds (icon, label, open / minimised marker); "Reset layout" stays its last item. Auto-placed windows look like any window; nothing announces the choice. **DM view / player view:** same rules; each scores only the tokens it draws.
- **Sprint 5 zones** (no new styling; the UI / art sprint BL-29 restyles later): right column = Initiative (DM) / sheet (player); bottom strip = the wide Game log; toasts and roll popups: players' bottom-right toast corner, the DM's top-centre region (as built, #256); top strip right of the rail = the active tool bar ([FR-MAP-60](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements)), which pushes windows right (FR-SHELL-55); left column = launcher-opened windows, then beside them, inward (#256); the rest = map. An auto-minimised window (FR-SHELL-47) looks like any minimised one (launcher ring); nothing announces it. **DM view / player view:** same rules and zones; the player's Initiative sits at the left edge above the Game log.

**Sprint 5 default layouts** (FR-SHELL-50, decided #256, token-free input; x, y, w × h in viewport px; usable area x 12…vw − 12, y 60…vh − 76; rail clear x ≥ 68; toast boxes FR-SHELL-49)

| Viewport | DM `cbt.initiative` | DM `dice.log` | Player `char.sheet` | Player `dice.log` | Player `cbt.initiative` (token-free; at Start: left edge above the log, y by token cover) | Player toast corner (hard) | DM top-centre box (soft) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1280×720 | 868, 60, 400×500 | 68, 484, 690×160 | 948, 60, 320×338 | 68, 484, 510×160 | 68, 60, 280×400 | 908, 410, 356×238 | 462, 100, 356×238 |
| 1280×800 | 868, 60, 400×500 | 68, 564, 690×160 | 948, 60, 320×418 | 68, 564, 510×160 | 68, 60, 280×400 | 908, 490, 356×238 | 462, 100, 356×238 |
| 1440×900 | 1028, 60, 400×500 | 68, 584, 1150×240 | 1108, 60, 320×440 | 68, 544, 850×280 | 68, 60, 280×400 | 1068, 590, 356×238 | 542, 100, 356×238 |
| 1600×900 | 1188, 60, 400×500 | 68, 584, 1150×240 | 1268, 60, 320×440 | 68, 544, 850×280 | 68, 60, 280×400 | 1228, 590, 356×238 | 622, 100, 356×238 |

- Growing windows (FR-SHELL-52): DM Initiative grows to the work area's bottom at 1280 × 720 / × 800 and to 572 at 900 px high (the Game log); the player's Initiative to ≤ 12 px above the Game log.
- **Tool bar push** (FR-SHELL-55): player at 1280×800 opens AoE → the panel at its home spot right of the rail, Initiative (68, 60, 280×400) pushed to x = the panel's right edge + 12, same y and size (the sheet at 948 is not reached). DM at 1440×900 with Scenes open picks the Fog tool → Scenes pushed right of the fog bar's wrapped rect.
- **Launcher-opened windows at 1440×900, DM, Initiative + Game log open** (the sim-8 FAIL j sequence; #256: OQ-SHELL-38 A, -30 A): Compendium → 68, 60, 440×512 (left edge, `minOpenH` 400 ≤ the 512 band); Party → inward at 520, 60, 440×512 (beside Compendium) or 576, 60 (beside Initiative), whichever covers fewer tokens (520 with none drawn; both sit partly under the DM's soft top-centre box, FR-SHELL-45 (4)); Encounters → auto-minimises Compendium, opens at 68, 60, 440×512. At 1280×720 the band above the log is 412 high: Compendium 68, 60, 440×412; Party auto-minimises it, and so on. Creatures (340 wide) and Scenes (260) fit side by side at the left edge at every size.
- **Sprint 6** (no new styling): a reopened window that had to move looks like any auto-placed one; nothing announces it (`data-placed="reopened"` for walkthroughs). Default layouts: §7's sprint-5 table, unchanged (token-free input). Per-kind default sizes, anchors and min sizes: [protocol §24](../protocol/README.md#24-combat-windows) / §18, unchanged; CP's and PF's new windows follow FR-SHELL-61. **DM view / player view:** same rules; each counts only the tokens it draws (DM incl. hidden; players never hidden ones). The toast and Fit changes are DM-only (FR-NOTE-42, FR-MAP-80); the reveal refit matters mostly to players (FR-MAP-82).

**Sprint 6 regions** (four reference sizes; x, y, w × h in px; FR-SHELL-48, -59, [FR-NOTE-42](notifications.md#6-functional-requirements), [FR-MAP-60](scenes-map.md#6-functional-requirements))

| Viewport | Players' toast corner (hard: placement + fit) | DM top-centre box (soft: placement only; fit: the summary strip + the shown stack under OQ-MAP-57 D, the shown stack only under C) | DM stack home, 320 wide (FR-NOTE-42) | DM tool bar home, max width (hard while shown) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1280×720 | 908, 410, 356×238 | 462, 100, 356×238 | 480, 100 | 62, 56, ≤ 392 (ends 454 < 462) |
| 1280×800 | 908, 490, 356×238 | 462, 100, 356×238 | 480, 100 | 62, 56, ≤ 392 |
| 1440×900 | 1068, 590, 356×238 | 542, 100, 356×238 | 560, 100 | 62, 56, ≤ 472 (ends 534 < 542) |
| 1600×900 | 1228, 590, 356×238 | 622, 100, 356×238 | 640, 100 | 62, 56, ≤ 552 (ends 614 < 622) |

- The tool bar's max width already stops 8 px before the DM box (FR-MAP-60), so a stack that stays home never meets it; sim-9's collision came from the stack leaving home for x 68 (FR-NOTE-42 stops that; FR-NOTE-43 covers a stack shifted left of a moved Initiative).
- **Reopen at a smaller size** (sim-9 a04, FR-SHELL-56): Compendium, Party, Encounters closed at 1440×900 and reopened at 1280×720 → each shown rect is fitted (FR-SHELL-29) and tested; one over Initiative, the Game log or another reopened window is re-placed (`reopened`), one in a clear spot stays (`stored`). Exact rects: FR-SHELL-62's golden tables.
- **No new dependency**: pointer events + CSS transform. A drag/resize library needs the DM's OK (AGENTS §3).
- **Defaults** (DM): `camp.players` top-right, `map.scenes` top-left (scenes-map). Player: none open in Scenes phase; Combat specs set `defaultOpen` for theirs (OQ-SHELL-06).
- **States:** `connecting` = skeleton over the page; `live` = shell; `reconnecting` = last view + banner, windows usable; `waiting` / `rejected` = campaign-management screens; nothing to show = base empty state.
- **DM view:** switcher + presence; DM window types; base = viewed scene. **Player view:** name + campaign; player window types; base = active scene.
- **Test IDs** (`ui-` per AGENTS §9.5): `ui-topbar`, `ui-presence-badge`, `ui-tool-rail`, `ui-launcher`, `ui-launcher-button` + `data-record-id=<kind>`, `ui-launcher-menu`, `ui-launcher-reset`, `ui-window` + `data-record-id=<kind>:<instance>` + `data-state`, `ui-window-title`, `-minimise`, `-close`, `-resize`, `ui-base-empty`, `ui-alert-flash` (+ `data-alert-id`), ~~`ui-sound-button` (+ `data-state`), `ui-sound-mute`, `ui-sound-volume`, `ui-sound-test`~~ *(retired by PF, FR-SHELL-73: sound moves into Preferences)*; test mode `shell-test-alert`; `ui-tooltip` (tooltip content); `ui-presence-badge` gains `data-state` = `known` \| `unknown`. *(N1)* `ui-launcher-activity` (inside `ui-launcher-button`); a minimised window keeps `ui-window` with `data-state="minimised"` and `hidden`. Indicator keeps live-sync's `sync-connection-status`. *(Sprint 4)* `ui-launcher` attr `data-compact` (`true` when icon-only), `ui-launcher-overflow` (the menu trigger, `data-count`), `ui-launcher-overflow-item` + `data-record-id=<kind>` + `data-state`; `ui-window` attr `data-placed` = `auto` \| `stored` \| `default` (how its current rect was chosen, for walkthroughs). *(Sprint 5)* `ui-window` attr `data-auto-minimised="true"` while auto-minimised (FR-SHELL-47; cleared on restore; client memory) and `data-placed` gains `pushed` (FR-SHELL-55); `ui-play-page` attr `data-keep-outs` (FR-SHELL-48); the toast region's mount `ui-toast-corner` (both clients; `data-rect` = "x,y,w,h" of `toastKeepOut`). *(Sprint 6)* `data-placed` gains `reopened` (FR-SHELL-56); auto-placed / re-placed `ui-window` gains `data-pc-clear="true"` when no PC footprint is under it (FR-SHELL-57); `ui-toast-corner` (DM) gains `data-shown` = "x,y,w,h" of `shownStack` or absent (FR-SHELL-59, only under OQ-MAP-57 D / C).
- **Sprint 6 (track PF): identity chip and Preferences** (no new styling; shadcn Button, Switch, Slider, Separator; the UI / art sprint BL-29 restyles later):
  - **Top bar, right** after the sprint: DM = presence Badge · connection indicator · **chip "● DM ⚙"**; player = connection indicator · **chip "● Player 1 ⚙"** (`volume-x` after the name while muted). The chip is a ghost Button, same height as the indicator; the dot has a 1 px dark ring so a light colour reads on the bar.
  - **Preferences window** (360 × 560): heading per section in the existing `text.muted` token at the shell's usual small heading size (as the Party window's section titles; no new style, rulings-b 26), sections separated by a Separator, in `order`: **Your colour** (CAMP's picker, FR-CAMP-35) · **Sound** (player: Mute, Volume, Test, locked hint) · **Dice** (DICEX's settings panel, if OQ-SHELL-47 A) · **Layout** (Reset layout, one line of help: "Puts every window back where it starts.") · **Account** (DM: Log out, if OQ-SHELL-47 A). Server-backed controls show the usual disabled state while not `live`.
  - **DM view:** colour (the DM colour), dice, layout, account; no Sound while the DM has no alert (OQ-SHELL-49). **Player view:** colour (own), sound, dice, layout.
  - **Test IDs:** `ui-prefs-open` (the chip; `data-state` = `closed` \| `open` \| `minimised` (FR-SHELL-70); `data-sound-state` = `on` \| `muted` \| `locked` on players, absent on the DM), `ui-prefs-chip-dot` (`data-color` = the hex drawn), `ui-prefs-window` (the body inside `ui-window` `shell.prefs:main`), `ui-prefs-section` + `data-record-id` = section id, `ui-prefs-reset-layout`. The sound controls keep `ui-sound-mute`, `ui-sound-volume`, `ui-sound-test`; `ui-sound-button` retires (FR-SHELL-73). *(PF1 build, integrator)* Also `ui-prefs-chip-sound`, `ui-sound-locked`.
- **Sprint 7 (track FU)** (no new styling): a auto-minimised window looks like any minimised one (launcher ring, FR-SHELL-37); nothing announces it (`data-auto-minimised-for` for walkthroughs). A returned window just moves back when the tool closes (no animation beyond today's). Keyboard: the focus ring is the shadcn default; a Tooltip opened by focus looks like one opened by hover. **DM view / player view:** same rules; each counts only the tokens it draws.

## 8. Data model

No server entity, record type or migration (#121). Client-side only:

| Entity | Field | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Layout *(browser `localStorage`)* | key | `pp.layout.v1.<campaignId>.<dm \| playerId>` | One per (origin, campaign, owner); no secret token in the key |
| | windows | WindowState[] | zod-parsed on read (FR-SHELL-17); ≤ ~4 KB |
| WindowState *(in `windows`)* | kind, instance, state, x, y, w, h, z | string, string (`main`), `open` \| `minimised` \| `closed`, int ×5 | px from viewport top-left; z = stacking order |
| | area *(#171)* | `{w, h}` int, optional | Work area when this window's geometry was last set (FR-SHELL-29) |
| | fixedH *(S2)* | `true`, optional | Set at a user resize end on a `growToContent` window (FR-SHELL-39); absent = grows; older entries still parse |
| | userSpot *(sprint 7, iff OQ-SHELL-55 B)* | `true`, optional | Set at a user move / resize end; cleared when the shell places the window (auto, pushed, returned, default); absent = the shell chose the spot (FR-SHELL-56 note); older entries parse as absent |
| Window type *(client code)* | FR-SHELL-07 fields; *(N1)* `activity?(store)`; *(sprint 4)* `restoreOpen?: false`; *(sprint 5)* `defaultSize: Size \| (vp) => Size`, `previousDefaults?: Size[]`; *(sprint 7)* `usefulSize?: Size` | — | Per-client registry; not sent or stored (FR-SHELL-38, -42, -48, -51, -53, -79) |
| Pre-push rect *(client memory, sprint 7)* | windowKey → Rect | — | FR-SHELL-81; dropped on reload, move, resize, close, minimise |
| WindowState *(sprint 8)* | userSize | `true`, optional | FR-SHELL-100 |
| Displacement memory *(client memory, sprint 8)* | windowKey → `{rect, userSpot?, userSize?, cause}` | — | FR-SHELL-102; replaces the pre-push rect |
| Overlay, top-bar item, chrome keep-out *(client code, sprint 8)* | registry entries | — | FR-SHELL-112, -113; append-only |
| WindowState *(sprint 9)* | `userSpot`, `userSize` | unchanged fields, new meaning | Either set = **remembered** (FR-SHELL-144); cleared only by Reset (FR-SHELL-153); no new field, old entries parse (FR-SHELL-154) |
| Window type *(client code, sprint 9)* | `absorbs?`, `onAbsorb?(fromKind, you)` | `string[]`, function | FR-SHELL-155; not sent or stored |
| Displacement memory *(client memory, sprint 9)* | cause `toolbar:<id>` only for remembered windows | — | FR-SHELL-150; `viewport:` / `window:` causes only for untouched windows |
| Keep-out region *(client memory, sprint 5)* | id, rect, kind | string (`toasts`, `map.toolbar`), Rect, `hard` \| `soft` | FR-SHELL-48; recomputed, never stored |
| Sound settings *(browser `localStorage`)* | key; muted, volume | `pp.sound.v1.<campaignId>.<playerId>`; bool, int 0–100 | FR-SHELL-24; defaults unmuted, 70 |
| Sound *(client code)* | id, label, play | — | FR-SHELL-26; MVP `ping` only |
| Preference section *(client code, sprint 6)* | id, title, order, available?, Component | `<prefix>.<name>`, string, int, `(store) => boolean`, React component | FR-SHELL-72; per-client registry; not sent or stored |

- *(Sprint 6)* **Where each setting lives** (FR-SHELL-76; no new key, record or field from SHELL):

| Setting | Lives in | Key / record | Owner spec |
|---|---|---|---|
| Player colour, DM colour | server (person) | `player.color`, `campaign.dmColor` (`#rrggbb`, FR-CAMP-30) | campaign-management |
| Sound: mute, volume | browser (device) | `pp.sound.v1.<campaignId>.<playerId>` | play-shell |
| Dice display, others' rolls, roll popups | browser (device) | `pp.dicex.v2.<campaignId>.<dm \| playerId>` | dice-extras |
| Dice look | server (person) | `dice-look` record | dice-extras |
| Layout (reset) | browser (device) | `pp.layout.v1.…` | play-shell |

- **Relations:** key names a Campaign and Player ([campaign-management](campaign-management.md)) by ID only; kinds name window types, not records. **Derived:** rendered geometry = clamp(fit(stored, area, work area), viewport) (FR-SHELL-29, -12); launcher states; presence known iff `live`; *(R1)* free area from the rendered rects, appearance counter (FR-SHELL-34); *(N1)* activity value at minimise + dot, client memory only (FR-SHELL-38); *(sprint 4)* placement scores, candidate free rectangles, launcher compaction (client memory, recomputed). No new stored field: an auto-placed rect is written as an ordinary `WindowState` geometry. *(Sprint 5)* Keep-out regions, the auto-minimised mark, viewport classes and the fitted default rects are client memory, recomputed; an auto-minimised window is stored as an ordinary `minimised` state; the layout entry's shape is unchanged (old entries parse). *(Sprint 6)* "Covered" (FR-SHELL-56), PC / token footprints (FR-SHELL-57, -58) and the shown stack rect (FR-SHELL-59) are derived, client memory; a re-placed reopen is written as an ordinary geometry; no stored or sent field changes. *(Sprint 7)* Auto-minimise order, useful sizes, `data-auto-minimised-for` and pre-push rects are derived / client memory; a auto-minimised window is stored as an ordinary `minimised` state, a returned one as an ordinary geometry; only `userSpot` (iff OQ-SHELL-55 B) is new in the entry. **Persisted runtime state:** the Layout entry; window manager state is client memory, rebuilt from it on load.

## 9. API & events

- **REST, WS intents, record types, domain events, signals:** none. Envelopes, rejects, close codes: [live-sync](live-sync.md).
- **Visibility:** N/A; layouts never leave the browser. Window contents read the synced store, already filtered server-side.
- **Client API for features:** `registerWindow(def)` (one line in the client's registry list), `openWindow(kind, instance?)` (*sprint 4:* `openWindow(kind, instance?, {by?})`, default `"user"`: click-driven callers need no change; self-driven opens pass `"feature"`, FR-SHELL-08), `useShellAlert()` (FR-SHELL-22), *(sprint 4)* `freeAreas` / `useFreeArea().areas` (FR-SHELL-44), *(sprint 5)* `keepOuts(client, vp)`, `reportKeepOut(id, rect | null)`, `useKeepOuts()` (`keep-outs.ts`, FR-SHELL-48); *(sprint 6)* `reportShownStack(id, rect | null)`, `shownStack(client)` (`toast-region.ts`, FR-SHELL-59, only under OQ-MAP-57 D / C); `openWindow` unchanged (the reopen rule lives in the manager, FR-SHELL-56). The shell passes no data. Alerts are client-side reactions to events the client already received; the shell adds no event, intent or record.
- *(Sprint 6)* **No new intent, event, record type or signal from SHELL.** Sections that write the server use their owners' intents (`camp.setColor`, FR-CAMP-33; `dicex.setLook`, FR-DICEX-26) with those events' visibility (every connection of the campaign). Client API: `PreferencesWindow` + `PrefSection` (FR-SHELL-72); `openWindow("shell.prefs")` from anywhere (e.g. a later "More in Preferences" link).

## 10. Permissions

| Action | DM | Player | Server check |
|---|---|---|---|
| Arrange, persist, reset own layout | ✓ | ✓ | — (browser only) |
| Open a window type | DM types | player types | None needed: registry is per client; data stays filtered server-side |
| Mute, volume | — | own browser | — (browser only) |

*(Sprint 5)* No permission change: placement, auto-minimising and keep-outs are per browser; nothing reaches the server. *(Sprint 6)* None either: reopen rule, footprints, stack rect and refits are per browser.

*(Sprint 7)* No permission change: parking, returns and key handling are per browser.

*(Sprint 8)* No permission change: the placer, zones, returns and the harness are per browser / test-only.

*(Sprint 9)* No permission change: window memory, z-order and Reset are per browser (own layout only); Party Manager's tabs keep their owners' permissions ([party-board §10](party-board.md#10-permissions)).

*(Sprint 6)* Open own Preferences: DM ✓, player ✓ (browser only). Each section keeps its owner's permissions (colour: own or DM any, FR-CAMP-33; dice look: own; device settings: own browser).

## 11. Edge cases & failure modes

- **Player / DM disconnect / reconnect:** last view + banner; windows still movable and written; players unaffected by DM drops. Snapshots don't move windows (FR-SHELL-16).
- **Campaign switch:** DM restores the new campaign's DM entry (default if none); players get the waiting screen, then the shell as it was.
- **Server crash:** layout unaffected (browser-side). **Browser crash:** every finished change was already written; a drag in progress is lost.
- **Another browser, fresh profile, or new origin** (dev vs verify port, new tunnel URL): separate storage → default layout. Accepted (#121).
- **Two tabs, same owner:** last write wins, no live jumps (FR-SHELL-19). **Two players on one browser** (different links): separate keys.
- **Viewport change:** clamp, render-only (FR-SHELL-12). **Removed or unavailable window type:** FR-SHELL-18. **Corrupt or full storage:** FR-SHELL-17.
- **Shared computer:** entries hold only window geometry and kinds, no game data.
- **Playtest pass:** viewport shrinks mid-session → windows refit at once, render-only; a drag / resize in progress is refit at pointer up. Layout from a bigger screen opened on a laptop → fits; reopened on the big screen → as stored. Tooltip pending when the connection drops: unaffected (client only). Presence: disconnect → "unknown" at once; reconnect → snapshot presence; server crash → "unknown" until rejoin (no stale "2/2 online").
- **Polish pass:** free area is recomputed from what is rendered (viewport resize, window moves); only the canvas decides when to use it (FR-MAP-44). Every window minimised → the work area. Disconnect / reconnect / crash: client-only geometry, unaffected; snapshots never open, close or move windows (FR-SHELL-16); a kind that first becomes available in a reconnect snapshot counts as one appearance (FR-SHELL-34).
- **Minimised (N1):** reload or a browser crash → the window comes back minimised and mounted fresh (its unsaved state is lost, like any reload); the dot baseline is taken after the first snapshot, so nothing old lights it. Disconnect / reconnect: the window stays mounted; a reconnect snapshot that changes `activity` (e.g. log entries made while offline) lights the dot: there is news. Server crash: same as reconnect. Snapshots never minimise, restore or close windows (FR-SHELL-16). Two tabs: dots are per page.
- **Sprint 4 (placement, launcher):**
  - Disconnect / reconnect: snapshots never open, close or move windows (FR-SHELL-16); a kind that first becomes available in a reconnect snapshot is an appearance and is auto-placed (FR-SHELL-40), scored on the tokens drawn after that snapshot. Server crash: same as reconnect; nothing server-side.
  - Reload: the default layout and stored spots apply as before; `restoreOpen: false` windows load closed; auto placement scores 0 until the canvas has drawn tokens (first load), so the default rect wins and the camera fit frames the tokens around it.
  - Two tabs, same owner: auto-placed rects are written like any change (last write wins, FR-SHELL-19).
  - No candidate free of other windows: today's cascade (protocol §18), still least token cover first. All candidates cover tokens equally (e.g. at the 48-px floor on 1280×720): today's order.
  - Viewport shrinks mid-session: the launcher recompacts at once; windows fit per FR-SHELL-29 (render-only; no re-placement).
- **Sprint 5 (windows, keep-outs, toast regions, tool bar push):**
  - Disconnect / reconnect: snapshots never open, close, move or auto-minimise windows (FR-SHELL-16); an appearance in a reconnect snapshot is auto-placed by FR-SHELL-45 with the keep-outs of that moment; toasts and popups keep their regions. Server crash: same as reconnect; nothing server-side.
  - Reload: stored layout as before (auto-minimised windows load `minimised`, no `data-auto-minimised`); default-sized windows follow the viewport (FR-SHELL-53); the tool bar re-reports after the first canvas render, so an auto placement before that sees only the toast box; a tool bar shown after a reload pushes again (FR-SHELL-55).
  - Viewport resize mid-session: windows fit render-only (FR-SHELL-29), nothing re-placed or auto-minimised; the toast boxes and the tool bar follow the viewport; a tool bar that grows (wraps) on a smaller viewport pushes again. A layout stored on a bigger screen may render windows over each other on a smaller one (render-only fit, accepted as today; Reset layout gives the named layout).
  - Screen full with only protected windows open (e.g. 1280×720 with the DM's Initiative grown by the user): FR-SHELL-45's last resort; nothing is auto-minimised that the user can't restore from the launcher.
  - Two tabs: auto-minimising and placement are per page; writes last-write-wins (FR-SHELL-19).
  - A keep-out the user moved a window onto: kept (user's choice); only auto placement avoids it.
- **Sprint 6 (reopen rule, footprints, shown stack, reveal refit):**
  - Disconnect / reconnect: snapshots never open, close or move windows (FR-SHELL-16), so no reopen happens; tokens that come into sight in a reconnect snapshot count as a reveal for a `fitted` camera (FR-MAP-82: one refit after that snapshot). Toast stacks re-place as built. Server crash: same as reconnect; nothing server-side.
  - Reload: the layout applies as stored (no re-placement on load, FR-SHELL-56 (6)); the first reopen after it applies the rule. The shown stack starts empty, so the first Fit after a reload avoids only what is showing (FR-SHELL-59).
  - Reopen while the canvas has drawn no tokens yet (first frames after load): footprints empty, so only windows and hard regions decide "covered" and the rank (as sprint 4's first-load note).
  - A reopen that finds the screen full auto-minimises the least recently used **other** user-opened window, never the one being opened; with only protected windows left, FR-SHELL-45's last resort.
  - Two tabs, same owner: a re-placed rect is written like any change (last write wins, FR-SHELL-19).
  - A window the user drags onto another stays there while open; only its next reopen moves it (FR-SHELL-56 (6)).
- **Sprint 7 (parking, Reset layout, returns, keys):**
  - Disconnect / reconnect: snapshots never open, close, move or auto-minimise windows (FR-SHELL-16); an appearance in a reconnect snapshot runs FR-SHELL-77 with the tokens drawn after it. Server crash: same as reconnect; nothing server-side.
  - Reload: auto-minimised windows load `minimised` (no `data-auto-minimised-for`); pre-push rects are gone, so a window pushed before the reload stays at its stored (pushed) rect; Reset layout after a reload is unchanged (named rects + Fit).
  - Tokens not drawn yet (first frames): no PC is known, so nothing auto-minimises for a PC; the first placement after the canvas draws applies the rule.
  - A PC walks under an open window: nothing moves (placement runs only on opens); its next reopen re-places it iff OQ-SHELL-55 B and the spot was the shell's.
  - The user restores a auto-minimised window: a user open (FR-SHELL-56); if its spot is now taken it is re-placed and may auto-minimise the next one in order; a window never auto-minimises itself.
  - Only protected windows open (1280×720, a grown Initiative): nothing to auto-minimise → FR-SHELL-77 steps (d)–(e); the map's refit clears what it can.
  - Two tabs, same owner: parks and returns are per page; writes last-write-wins (FR-SHELL-19).
  - Space held while a control is focused: the control gets it (no pan); Space-pan resumes when focus returns to the body or the canvas (click on the map).
- **Sprint 8 (one placer):** *disconnect / reconnect / crash*: snapshots never move windows; a kind first available in a reconnect snapshot is an `appear`. *Reload*: the written layout is the settled one; memory is gone, so displaced windows grow toward their target at the next `viewport` / `return`; zones re-apply on `load`. *Tokens not drawn yet*: no PCs; `load` settles again after the first token draw (FR-SHELL-95). *A `moved` camera*: zones fall back to the ladder (FR-SHELL-99). *An overlay blocking input*: settles queue until it goes. *Two tabs*: per page, last write wins.
- **Sprint 9 (window memory, Party Manager):**
  - *Disconnect / reconnect:* snapshots never open, close or move windows (FR-SHELL-16); a kind first available in a reconnect snapshot is an `appear`: a remembered one comes up at its stored rect (FR-SHELL-146), an untouched one by the ladder. *Server crash:* nothing server-side; layouts unaffected; after restart the reconnect rule above.
  - *Reload:* remembered windows at their stored rects, stored z; displacement memory is gone, so a window pushed when the page reloaded stays at its pushed rect until the user moves it or the bar shows and goes again (as built for pushes; it is still remembered). Absorbed `party.summary` entries migrate once (FR-SHELL-155).
  - *Viewport smaller than a remembered window's stored size:* rendered shrunk to the work area (never below min), stored size kept; a window wider than the viewport at min keeps FR-SHELL-12's clamp.
  - *A remembered window over a tool bar's home* when the tool opens: pushed by the least move (FR-SHELL-150), back after.
  - *Everything remembered* (the DM moved every launcher window): an untouched window that opens finds no clear spot → L4 / L5, then FR-SHELL-45's last resort over a remembered window, on top; nothing remembered moves.
  - *Two remembered windows at the same rect:* both kept; the most recent on top.
  - *Reset while a tool bar is shown:* Reset writes the defaults, then the bar pushes the untouched windows under it as built.
  - *Party Manager:* stored tab `companions` or none → Party status ([FR-PARTY-60](party-board.md#611-sprint-9-party-manager-track-pm)); an old layout with `party.summary` open and Party Manager open → Party Manager kept as it was, the summary entry dropped.
  - *Two tabs, same owner:* last write wins (FR-SHELL-19); each page fronts its own windows.
- **Alerts:** audio still locked (no interaction yet) → flash + Toast only; the speaker shows `locked`. Two tabs of one player → both alert and play (accepted). Tab hidden → title mark (FR-SHELL-23); the sound plays if unlocked. Disconnect / reconnect: alerts come only from live events, so nothing replays on a snapshot (callers' rule, e.g. FR-CBT-41). Server crash: no effect on settings. Corrupt or missing sound settings → defaults.
- **Sprint 6 (Preferences):**
  - *Disconnect:* the chip and window stay usable; Sound, Layout and the dice device settings keep working (browser-only); colour Apply and Dice look Save are disabled (their FRs) and the window shows live-sync's banner. *Reconnect:* the snapshot refreshes the colour and the chip's dot; nothing replays. *Server crash:* browser settings unaffected; a colour pick in flight is the CAMP crash rule (FR-CAMP-33).
  - *Reload:* the window loads closed (`restoreOpen: false`); every setting comes back from its own store. *Old layout entry* with no `shell.prefs` → nothing to restore. *Old sound entry* (`pp.sound.v1`) → read as before.
  - *Two tabs, same owner:* device settings last write wins (as today); the other tab picks up a sound change at its next alert (same key), a dice change through the `storage` event where that store already listens.
  - *Campaign switch:* the window shows the new campaign's settings (keys are per campaign); an unapplied colour draft is dropped.
  - *Chip name:* a rename shows ≤ 1 s (player record); unknown player row (deleted, old client) → "Player" and the role accent dot (FR-CAMP-36 fallback).
  - *Small viewport* (1280×720): the window fits the work area (FR-SHELL-29), never below 320 × 280; the body scrolls; WF's placement decides where it opens (`minOpenH` 400, seams S2, S3).

## 12. Performance & bandwidth

- No network cost. One synchronous ≤ 4 KB `localStorage` write per finished change.
- Alerts: one CSS animation on one element; the ping is synthesised (no download). Sound settings ≤ 50 B per write.
- Drag/resize moves the window by CSS transform; no store write or canvas re-render until pointer up; closed windows are unmounted ~~and minimised~~ (*N1:* minimised ones stay mounted, `hidden`; ≤ ~10 window types per client, so the cost is their store subscriptions; `activity` is one O(1) read per store change). Target *(proposed, OQ-SHELL-10)*: canvas pan/zoom ≥ 50 fps with 8 open windows on the DM's laptop.
- *(Sprint 4)* Auto placement: ≤ ~10 candidates × ≤ 200 token boxes per auto open (O(n), only on open); `freeAreas` on window change / resize (≤ ~10 windows); launcher compaction measures button widths on resize and availability change only. No network, no stored-size change (the layout entry stays ≤ ~4 KB).
- *(Sprint 5)* **CON-44: 0 B.** Nothing enters the snapshot or any frame (layouts, keep-outs, auto-minimising and default sizes are browser-only, #121), so no probe was run (brief: measure only what enters the snapshot or traffic). Placement with inward columns: ≤ (3 + 2 × 10) columns × (4 + 4 × 10) rows ≈ 1,000 spots × ≤ 12 overlap tests, only on an auto open (< 2 ms; asserted ≤ 5 ms in `FR-SHELL-46 inward column`). Auto-minimising re-runs placement ≤ 10 times. Coverage figures in FR-SHELL-54 and the fit scales in [MAP §12](scenes-map.md#12-performance--bandwidth) are spec-time geometry computations (recomputed after DM gate 1, #256: OQ-SHELL-32 C, the DM's 356×238 top-centre box excluded from the fit; throwaway script over the §7 table and the `session-sim-8` Goblin Ambush token cells; not committed); the build's registry-driven tests are the authority.
- *(Sprint 5)* **Sizing notes** (#256): both boxes hold **1** toast + 1 popup (238 px); a second toast (286) would end the player's sheet at 370 at 1280×800. A stack taller than its box grows while it lasts (the DM's top-centre stack keeps clear of Initiative as built, FR-NOTE-32). Coverage (OQ-SHELL-39 A, §7 layouts): DM 62.1 / 64.7 / 57.1 / 62.8 %, player 65.4 / 63.4 / 56.9 / 65.6 % at 1280×720 / 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900; every floor holds (DM 1280×720 +2.1, 1440×900 +2.1; player 1280×720 +0.4, 1440×900 +1.9). The DM's top-centre box costs the fit: cells with every token visible, DM Game log + Initiative 23 / 24 / 28 / 33 px, + Scenes 16 / 24 / 22 / 22 px (FR-MAP-59; below the 24 px floor → PCs first, OQ-SHELL-40).
- *(Sprint 5)* **CON-44 ceilings** (#256): DM **1.5 MiB**, players 512 KiB; track W adds 0 B.
- *(Sprint 6)* **CON-44: 0 B.** Nothing enters the snapshot or any frame (reopen rule, footprints, stack rect, refits are browser-only), so no snapshot probe was run. Cost: the "covered" test is ≤ 20 rect overlaps per user open; token footprints add candidate rows and columns (FR-SHELL-58, ≤ 200 boxes), capped at **4,000 spots** per auto open × ≤ 12 overlap tests (≈ 50 k tests, a few ms; asserted ≤ 5 ms best-of-5 in `FR-SHELL-58 token-free spot first` for 10 windows × 200 boxes, as sprint 5's inward-column bound); FR-SHELL-62's brute check is test-only: a 16-px grid (≈ 3.6 k spots × 2 sizes per open at 1280×720) with a 4-px refine around hits; the whole file ≤ 10 s.
- *(Sprint 6)* **Measured at spec time** (throwaway `bun test` probe over the built `fitScene` / `defaultLayout` / `freeAreas` with the `session-sim-8` Goblin Ambush cells, deleted before commit; [MAP §12](scenes-map.md#12-performance--bandwidth) has the Fit table): (a) DM Fit with today's 356×238 box as an obstacle: `pcs` 48 px at 1280×720 with the token rows from y 36 (under the 48-px top bar, sim-9 a03 reproduced), `all` 25 / 26 / 31 px at 1280×800 / 1440×900 / 1600×900, tokens confined to x 100–568 at 1440×900 (sim-9 a00's "left strip"); without the empty box: `all` 36 / 43 / 44 / 44 px. (b) Player at 1280×800 / 1280×720 / 1440×900, fit on the 4 tokens player 2 saw at the Show (r02), then 7 goblins / art tokens come into sight: 1 / 5 / 0 of the 7 land under a window or off-screen with no refit; a refit gives `all` at 48 / 43 / 48 px (FR-MAP-82).
- *(Sprint 6, review pass, rulings-b #6 / #12; 2026-10-20, second throwaway probe over the built `defaultLayout`, `reduce`, `renderedWindowRects`, `fitScene`, DM client, sim-8 cells at the windows-only Fit, deleted before commit)*:
  - **Reopen (OQ-SHELL-41)**: open the 9 non-default DM kinds at 1440×900, close them, reopen at 1280×720 / at 1440×900 (the built placer re-places; "covered" emulated per option): **today** (no re-place) 26 / 19 overlapping window pairs, PCs under windows 3 / 2 of 3; **A** 8 / 4 reopened, 1 / 5 kept, 0 overlaps, 0 PCs; **B** 8 / 5 reopened, 1 / 4 kept, 0 overlaps, 0 PCs; **C** 7 / 4 reopened, 2 / 5 kept, 0 overlaps, 0 PCs. Every option ends with 8 of the 9 auto-minimised (the screen holds one launcher window beside Initiative + Game log at these sizes).
  - **PC margin (OQ-SHELL-42)**: the launcher sequence at 1280×720 / 1440×900 with the PC rule as centre, footprint + 4 px, or ¾-visible (emulated with sample points on the built rank): **no difference on this cell set**: 8 auto-minimised, 9 / 8 shrunk, 0 PCs touched, 12 / 9 of 16 tokens under some window in all three. The options differ only when a PC sits exactly at a slot edge (sim-9 a01 / a05's live camera), which the unit cases pin.
  - **Tokens under the DM summary** (OQ-MAP-57) after a nothing-shown Fit: see [MAP §12](scenes-map.md#12-performance--bandwidth).
- *(Sprint 6, PF)* **SHELL adds 0 B:** the chip, window, sections and the moved sound settings are browser-only; no probe needed for them. The one PF change that reaches the wire (colour as `#rrggbb`) was **measured** in [CAMP §12](campaign-management.md#12-performance--bandwidth): −21 B to +14 B per snapshot frame. The window renders ≤ 6 sections; the colour picker's shading mask is recomputed per hue on a 32 × 32 grid (FR-CAMP-35): measured with the shared `colors.ts` in Bun on the dev laptop, 1.8 ms per hue for validity alone, 4.7 ms with the distance to 7 other colours (throwaway script, not committed), inside a 60 fps frame.

- *(Sprint 7)* **CON-44: 0 B.** Nothing enters the snapshot or any frame from SHELL (auto-minimising, useful sizes, returns, key handling are browser-only, #121), so no probe was run for SHELL (the track's only server-side change, BL-46, adds no field either: [CBT §12](combat-tracker.md#12-performance--bandwidth)). Cost: FR-SHELL-77 re-runs the existing placement at most once per auto-minimisable window plus one look-ahead per round (≤ 40 windows, FR-SHELL-17; typically ≤ 12 DM kinds), each ≤ 4,000 spots (FR-SHELL-58's cap): asserted ≤ 25 ms best-of-5 for 12 auto-minimisable windows × 200 token boxes and ≤ 100 ms for the 40-window worst case in `placement.bench.ts` (`bun run bench`, not `bun test`); FR-SHELL-87's brute checks stay test-only and inside the file's 10 s.

- *(Sprint 8)* **CON-44: 0 B.** Nothing reaches the server; `userSize` is browser-only (≤ 15 B per window). No probe needed. Cost: the built ranker per placed window × ≤ 2 rungs × 2 gaps × look-ahead, ≤ 4,000 spots each, + ≤ 1 `dryFit`; gate FR-SHELL-119.

- *(Sprint 9)* **CON-44: 0 B.** Nothing new reaches the server or the snapshot: window memory reuses `userSpot` / `userSize` (browser `localStorage`, #121), Party Manager's tabs read records already sent (party-board §12), `party.summary`'s removal deletes nothing on the wire. No probe needed (brief: measure only what persists server-side or enters the snapshot / traffic). Cost: a remembered window's open is O(1) (no ladder); the settle's remaining work only shrinks (FR-SHELL-145); BL-82's gate FR-SHELL-158 inside the full `bun run bench`. The layout entry stays ≤ ~4 KB (one fewer window kind).

- **Walkthrough per slice** (§13 sprint 7; rulings-b #5): **FU-combat** (FU0 + FU2, one builder) U7–U12, U14, U18; **FU1** U0–U4, U6, U18; **FU3** U5, U12 (marker client part), U13, U16; **FU4** U15, U17. The last FU slice to merge runs **U0–U18** in full.
- **FU1 → FU2 on `encounters-window.tsx`** (rulings-b #13): FU1 adds one `usefulSize` line to `cbt.encounters`' def; FU2 (FU-combat) rewrites the editor and End dialog in the same file and **rebases on FU1** for that line. The Start-dialog note (`cbt-enc-start-kept-note`, FR-CBT-141's client part) is FU2's (rulings-b #5).
- **Docs at consolidation:** protocol §18 (parking rule, park order, useful size, Reset layout, returns, push vs the DM box, Space guard), §24 (the size table incl. `usefulSize`), combat-tracker protocol rows (Start's kept HP, End's summoned tokens); glossary (OQ-SHELL-57). No data-model change (no column). **None:** events, intents, record types, wire bytes.
- **Dependency:** none. Parking reuses the hand-rolled placement (≤ 20 rects, ≤ 200 boxes); key handling and focus Tooltips use the existing Radix-based `Tooltip` and DOM events.

**Sprint 7, track ST:** slice **ST2** `build/st-window` appends one line to each of `src/client/dm/windows.ts` and `src/client/player/windows.ts` (append-only registries, AGENTS §9.9), its golden row and size-table row (FR-SHELL-86), and adds `@ui/features/stats/*`. It doesn't touch the frame, placement or keep-outs. It goes **after FU1** (and ST-core) merges, then rebases on it (seam S5).

| OQ-SHELL-42 | **How much margin around a PC's footprint?** (footprints decided, BL-39 / #285; only the margin is open) | **Decided (#288): A** (footprint + 4 px). Options: **A. The whole footprint + 4 px stays clear**: never a window edge on a PC. / B. ≥ ¾ of each PC's footprint stays visible: fewer moves, a window may clip a PC's rim. / C. Footprint + 16 px (room for the name label and a selection ring): the most map around a PC, more shrinking at 1280×720. **Measured** (§12, the launcher sequence at 1280×720 / 1440×900 on the sim-8 cells): no difference between A, B and today's centre rule on this cell set (8 auto-minimised, 0 PCs touched in each); the options differ only when a PC sits exactly at a slot edge (sim-9 a01 / a05), which the unit cases pin | — · decided (#288) | FR-SHELL-57 |
| OQ-SHELL-43 | **Players' sheet at 1280×800** (BL-42; root cause: the fit framed only the 4 tokens player 2 saw at the Show; goblins that came into sight later landed under the sheet, nothing refit) | **Decided (#288): A** (refit on reveal while fitted, players only). Options: **A. Refit on reveal while fitted** ("camera offset", FR-MAP-82, players only): the map re-frames once when a newly seen token lands under a window or off-screen; never after the player panned or zoomed (a pan or wheel cancels it at once), never mid-drag; the map moves by itself when a door opens. / B. Narrower sheet below 1440 wide (280 px): +40 px of map, but newly seen tokens can still land under it. / C. Docked sheet column (the canvas ends at the sheet's left edge while it is open): never under it, but reverses the §4 non-goal "docked panels" and costs a fixed 332 px column | — · decided (#288) | FR-SHELL-60, FR-MAP-82 |

Confirm nothing in fog's client delays or batches token arrivals in a way that would refit twice | **Settled by rulings-b 21:** no fog-of-war change; the reveal refit reads new token records in the player's store (fog already decides what arrives). Rejected: ~~fog adds a "sight settled" signal~~ | — · settled | fog-of-war |

**Sprint 7** (track FU). Decided by the DM before the spec (#310 (1) A, not open): auto-minimise the oldest launcher window instead of covering a PC when no spot is clear of both windows and PCs; protected default-open windows unchanged. **Bold** = suggested pick, not decided.

(#310 says "the oldest launcher window") | **Decided (#315): A** (least recently used launcher window). **A. The least recently used launcher window (lowest z, the one you looked at longest ago): what "oldest" means on screen, and the same pick as today's screen-full rule (FR-SHELL-47), so one rule** / B. The one opened first (open order; a client-memory open counter per window, reset to layout order on reload): literal "oldest", but a window you keep using goes first if you opened it early / C. The largest (descending rendered area): frees the most room in one go, but it is often the one you're reading (Compendium, Party) | no · owner: DM | FR-SHELL-78 |

A. Shrink first (as built): a window auto-minimises others only when even its min size has no clear, PC-free spot: fewest windows put away, but windows open small / **B. Useful size first: a window opens at least at its useful size (its `minOpenH`, else its default height up to 360, capped by the band above the Game log); below that, auto-minimise the oldest launcher window first; shrink below it only when nothing is left to auto-minimise: windows readable, a little more auto-minimising** / C. Return to the pre-push spot when the tool's bar goes, if you didn't move or resize the window meanwhile and the old spot is free; else it stays: the layout comes back by itself, your own moves win** / B. ## 16. Out of scope

- *(Sprint 9)* A per-window "pin" / "unpin" toggle or a way to forget one window's spot (Reset forgets all); layouts across browsers (#121); snapping or docking; a remembered window following a PC; announcing a put-away window; the toast runtime (FX); the command palette and shortcuts themselves (SP); the Reminders / Strongholds tabs and window (RS).
- *(Sprint 8)* Re-placing windows when a PC walks under one; server-side or cross-browser layouts (#121); announcing a put-away window; the TT / SB / CB surfaces (they register).
- §2's later list; docked panels, pop-outs, cross-browser layout (§4); window contents (owning specs).
- *(Sprint 7)* Re-placing windows when a PC walks under them (placement runs on opens only); announcing a auto-minimised window; auto-minimising protected windows; syncing layouts across browsers (#121). BL-52 (a window dragged under a shown tool bar) and BL-53 (tool rail over window content at 320 px) stay backlog items for idle slots.
- *(Sprint 6, retired by sprint 7 FR-SHELL-81 if OQ-SHELL-54 A)* Pushed windows returning after the tool closes (accepted for later, sprint-5 playtest).
- *(Sprint 6)* Re-placing windows on load or on a viewport resize (only reopens re-place, FR-SHELL-56 (6)); the Preferences window and the companion card themselves (PF, CP).
